# A variable-first-step threshold region excluded by the nested witness

Status: COMPLETE JOINT PROOF DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. Max4 round39, the first
of two analytical rounds under master_guidance78e668c9782fc528, completed
baseline38 and ceiling40. No fact_submit is authorized. This document is
not a fact, an accepted theorem, or a solution of the original Goal.

The only accepted input used in the certificate exclusion is
04219554e41d9750, Section10. Accepted1252b8823bb5a290 supplies the
interpolation orientation; the actual functions and all their rows are
also constructed directly below. No other mathematical assertion is
imported as a premise. In particular the earlier nested construction is
rederived on its enlarged domain, rather than extrapolated.

Audit warning, explicitly acknowledged: original43a616da7da451c7 has the
false first-increment bound identified by f0730b188bcec643 and main
guidance09f6c32b4d027b38. Its original file and hash are preserved. None
of its scalar positivity bounds, its old-system survivor assertion, or
its whole-box theorem is used in this proof. A local repair of that
scalar argument is not treated as acceptance of its complete package.

All reasoning below is symbolic. No numerical evaluation, solver,
machine algebra, replay, installation, consult, subagent or verifier
call was used.

## 1. Original certificate and target region

Let h=(u,w,z,v) be four nonnegative physical steps, and put

 s=u+w+z+v, C=1+s.

The original full primitive certificate has twenty nonnegative numbers
Lambda_ij for distinct i,j in{0,1,2,3,4}, with Lambda_ii=0. Define the
column e=(1,1,1,1,1)^T, the matrix T by T_ii=1,T_it=h_t for t<i and
T_it=0 for t>i, the flow matrix L_flow=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda, and

 omega=(-2u,-2w,-2z,-2v,2s)^T,
 Dprim=diag(u(2-u),w(2-w),z(2-z),v(2-v),-s(s+2)),
 M=Dprim+(L_flow T+T^T L_flow^T)/2.

The requirements are L_flow^T e=omega and M positive semidefinite on
all of R^5. Every original coordinate is retained, including at zero
steps. There is no sparsity, rank, reversal, or symmetry restriction.

For any actual gradient-descent trajectory of a convex 1-smooth
function, write X_i for the positions, G_i for the gradients, F_i for
the values, and define

 Q_ij=F_i-F_j+<G_i,X_j-X_i>+||G_i-G_j||^2/2,
 V_h=2sum_(i<4)h_i(F_i-F_4)
       +sum_(i<4)h_i(1-h_i)||G_i||^2-sC||G_4||^2.      (1)

The accepted identity from04219554e41d9750 is

 V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(G_0,...,G_4)>.  (2)

Here the matrix inner product is trace of the transpose times the
second matrix. Since every Q_ij<=0, an actual trajectory with V_h<0
excludes every such original certificate.

The region considered in this document is

 h>=0, C>=C0:=193/25, w<=4/3, 6w+7v<=18.             (3)

There is no fixed first step, no third-step interval or upper bound,
and no assumed ordering of the four steps. In particular (3) contains
the entire threshold part of w<=4/3,v<=10/7, as well as the additional
region v>10/7,w<3-7v/6. The conclusion will be that (3) has no full
primitive certificate. It is not a reduction of all threshold words
to this region.

## 2. The cut conditions, with their actual functions

For an index j in{0,1,2,3} put Pj=sum_(i<j)h_i and Rj=sum_(i>j)h_i.
Every full primitive certificate necessarily satisfies

 C(h_j-1)_+ <= (1+Pj)(1+Rj).                         (4)

To prove (4) when h_j>1, set A=1+Pj,B=1+Rj,r=(h_j-1)/B>0.
Use the single one-dimensional function

 F_cut(x)=max_(g in[-r,1]) (xg-g^2/2), X_0=A.

Its derivative is the projection of x onto[-r,1]. Up to query j the
positions are 1 plus the remaining prefix sum, so their derivative is1.
The selected update takes X_j=1 to-(h_j-1)=-Br. Every later position
is -r times one plus its remaining tail sum, so the derivative is-r
and X_4=-r. All sampled values equal X_i G_i-G_i^2/2.

For any saturated values F_i=<X_i,G_i>-||G_i||^2/2, expansion of the
updates gives

 V_h=||X_0||^2-||X_4+sG_4||^2.                     (5)

Indeed 2sum h_i<X_i,G_i>-sum h_i^2||G_i||^2 telescopes to
||X_0||^2-||X_4||^2; the remaining terms in(1) are
-2s<X_4,G_4>-s^2||G_4||^2. Thus the cut function has
V_h=A^2-C^2r^2, proving(4) by(2). When h_j<=1, (4) is automatic.
This construction includes zero steps elsewhere in the word. It has
an actual minimizer0 and is convex and1-smooth by the projection
argument given explicitly in the next section.

In particular the first and last cuts imply

 u<=2C/(C+1)<2, v<=2C/(C+1)<2.                      (6)

For a step at most1 these bounds are automatic, since C>=193/25>1.
For a step exceeding1 they follow by rearranging C(u-1)<=C-u, or
respectively C(v-1)<=C-v.

## 3. Complete phase domain of one nested planar family

For this section only, assume

 u>1, w>=0, z>1, v>=0,
 a=u-1, m=1+w, c=z-1, D=1+v,
 N=u+m, q=a/m>0, t=c/D>0,
 C=N+D+c=N+D(1+t).                                  (7)

No upper bound on u,w,z,v is needed here. Given an amplitude rho>=0,
define five base and auxiliary scalar positions and gradients by

 Xbase=(N,m,1,-c,-t), Gbase=(1,1,1,-t,-t),
 Xaux=(1,-a,-q,cq,tq), Gaux=(1,-q,-q,tq,tq),
 X_i=(Xbase_i,sqrt(rho)*Xaux_i),
 G_i=(Gbase_i,sqrt(rho)*Gaux_i),
 F_i=<X_i,G_i>-||G_i||^2/2.                          (8)

These are the original four physical updates. The base updates use
N-u=m,m-w=1,1-z=-c,-c+vt=-t. The auxiliary updates use
1-u=-a,-a+wq=-q,-q+zq=cq,cq-vtq=tq.

For the saturated values in(8), the residual is exactly

 Q_ij=<G_i-G_j,X_j-G_j>.                            (9)

The base target normals are(N-1,w,0,-vt,0); the auxiliary target
normals are(0,-wq,0,vtq,0). Here is the complete finite-row check,
organized by the target index j; omitted diagonal terms are zero.

 * Target0: sources1,2 give0; sources3,4 give-(1+t)(N-1).
 * Target1: source0 gives-rho*w*q*(1+q); source2 gives0;
   sources3,4 give-w*(1+t)*(1+rho*q^2).
 * Targets2 and4: every source gives0.
 * Target3: sources1,2 give-vt*(1+t)*(1+rho*q^2);
   source4 gives0; source0 gives

       Q_03=vt[-(1+t)+rho*q*(1-tq)].                 (10)

All twenty directed finite rows are therefore nonpositive exactly when

 0<=rho<=(1+t)/(q(1-tq))  if v>0 and tq<1;
 arbitrary rho>=0        if v=0 or tq>=1.             (11)

The equality cases v=0 and tq=1 are included. Necessity of this phase
condition is the single row(10), and sufficiency is the full list above.

Add the optimum jet X_star=G_star=F_star=0. All Q_(i,star) vanish.
For Q_(star,i)=-<G_i,X_i-G_i>, the respective inner products are

 N-1, w*(1+rho*q^2), 0, vt^2*(1+rho*q^2), 0,

which are all nonnegative. Thus every optimum row holds as well.

An explicit single static function is

 K_rho=conv{G_0,G_1,G_3},
 F_rho(X)=max_(G in K_rho)(<X,G>-||G||^2/2).         (12)

The other sampled gradients satisfy G_2=G_1 and G_4=G_3=-tG_1, so
0 belongs to K_rho. Equation(9), for every source vertex, is precisely
the condition <G-G_i,X_i-G_i><=0 for all G in K_rho. Completing the
square identifies the maximizer in(12) with the unique Euclidean
projection of X onto K_rho. Consequently it is G_i at X_i, and(12)
has the values F_i in(8).

To verify smoothness directly, the two projection inequalities at
points X,X' give <X-X',P(X)-P(X')>>=||P(X)-P(X')||^2, where P is
projection onto K_rho. Hence P is1-Lipschitz. The two maximizing
inequalities give

 0<=F_rho(X')-F_rho(X)-<P(X),X'-X>
     <=<P(X')-P(X),X'-X><=||X'-X||^2.

Thus F_rho is differentiable with gradient P, and is convex as a
maximum of affine functions of X. It is finite since K_rho is compact.
Finally 0 in K_rho implies F_rho>=0=F_rho(0). Its true minimizer set
is nonempty; no assertion of uniqueness or normalized payoff is needed.

By(5) and X_4=G_4, the exact primitive energy is

 V_h(rho)=N^2-C^2t^2+rho*(1-C^2t^2q^2).             (13)

This proves the complete all-amplitude test within the explicit family.
If v>0 and tq<1, it is equivalent to testing rho=0 and the upper
endpoint in(11), since(13) is affine in rho. If v=0 or tq>=1, it is
equivalent to both N^2-C^2t^2>=0 and1-C^2t^2q^2>=0. In particular
tq>=1 is impossible for a full certificate on(7), because C>1.
These are statements only about the family(8),(12); no classification
of all legal planar functions or all scalar trajectories is asserted.

## 4. Exact reduced energy in the relevant phase

Suppose0<tq<1 and choose the legal amplitude

 rho=(1+t)/(q(1-tq)).

It remains legal when v=0, even though that case also allows larger
amplitudes. Define

 k=(q*N^2-1/q)/(1+q)
   =u*(N-m/(u-1))
   =u^2+m*u-m-m/(u-1),
 Gred(u,m,C,D)=N^2-k*(1+t)-C^2*t^2,
 N=u+m, t=(C-N-D)/D.                                (14)

To check the formula for k, factor its numerator using
(u-1)N+m=u(N-1) and m+u-1=N-1. Direct expansion of(13) then gives

 V_h(rho)=(1+q)/(1-tq)*Gred(u,m,C,D).                (15)

The factor is positive. No feasibility of a formal covariance matrix
or deletion of an interpolation pair has entered this reduction.

## 5. All old-cut survivors in(3) enter this phase

Assume all cuts(4) hold for a word in(3). Then(6) gives u,v<2, and

 z=C-1-u-w-v > C0-19/3=104/75>1.                    (16)

Set m=1+w,D=1+v,N=u+m. These have1<=m<=7/3 and1<=D<3. The third
cut gives Ct<=N, or equivalently

 N>=fcut(C,D):=C(C-D)/(C+D).                         (17)

Here C>D. The function fcut increases in C and decreases in D,
as follows from

 partial_C fcut=1-2D^2/(C+D)^2>0,
 partial_D fcut=-2C^2/(C+D)^2<0.

Put D0=17/7. If D<=D0, then

 N>=fcut(C0,D0)=89359/22200>4,
 u=N-m>5/3.                                         (18)

If D>=D0, the last condition in(3) becomes

 m<=mline(D):=(31-7D)/6.

On D0<=D<3 we have5/3<mline(D)<=7/3. Furthermore
fcut(C0,D)-mline(D) is strictly increasing in D, since

 C0/(C0+D)<=1351/1776<16/21,
 d/dD [fcut(C0,D)-mline(D)]
      >=7/6-512/441=5/882>0.

Its value at D0 is greater than5/3 by(18). Thus in both cases

 5/3<u<2, 0<q=(u-1)/m<1, 0<t<=N/C<1.               (19)

This proves the required physical phase, including all potential zero
and small values of w or v. It also proves u>1 rather than assuming
away a first step at most1.

## 6. Reduction of the whole region to one rational corner

For5/3<u<2 and1<=m<=7/3, the coefficient k in(14) is positive.
Indeed its derivatives in m and u are

 k_m=u-1-1/(u-1)<0,
 k_u=2u+m+m/(u-1)^2>0,

so its minimum on the closed bounding rectangle is
k(5/3,7/3)=5/6>0. Wherever t>0, differentiation of(14) gives

 partial_C Gred=-k/D-2C*t^2-2C^2*t/D<0,
 partial_D Gred=(k+2C^2*t)*(C-N)/D^2>0,
 partial_m Gred=2N-k_m*(1+t)+k/D+2C^2*t/D>0.         (20)

Let Dhat=max(D,D0), and mhat=mline(Dhat). Conditions(3) imply
m<=mhat, D<=Dhat. The first cut also gives

 C>=Cmin(u):=max(C0,u/(2-u)).

Increasing m and D to mhat and Dhat, and decreasing C to Cmin, in
(20) therefore gives

 Gred(u,m,C,D)<=Gred(u,mline(Dhat),Cmin(u),Dhat).       (21)

All intermediate arguments of(20) have t>0. For example at the
smallest C, largest u and the final m,D one has

 t=(C0-u-31/6)/Dhat+1/6>79/225>0,

using u<2 and Dhat<3; earlier comparison points have larger C-N-D.
The coefficient k stays positive by the rectangle bound above.
The virtual comparison parameters need not satisfy every old cut;
only the proven sign conditions for(20) are used.

For each fixed Dhat in[D0,3], write m=mline(Dhat). The function on
the right of(21) has its maximum in5/3<u<2 at

 U0=2C0/(C0+1)=193/109.                              (22)

Here is a full derivative proof, retaining both sides of the first-cut
boundary. If5/3<u<=U0, then Cmin=C0. Since U0<9/5, the formulas give

 N>10/3, 2/5<t<3/5, k_u<12, C0>7, Dhat<=3.

For the lower t bound, C0-u-31/6>113/150 and thus
t>113/450+1/6=94/225>2/5. The upper bound follows by setting
u=5/3,Dhat=D0 in the decreasing expression
(C0-u-31/6)/Dhat+1/6, yielding226/425<3/5.
For k_u, use
2u+m+m/(u-1)^2<18/5+7/3+21/4=671/60<12.

The derivative of Gred in this interval is

 2N-k_u*(1+t)+k/Dhat+2C0^2*t/Dhat
   >20/3-96/5+196/15=8/15>0.                        (23)

If U0<=u<2, then Cmin=C_u:=u/(2-u). Since U0>7/4,

 C_u'=2/(2-u)^2>32,
 t_u=(C_u'-1)/Dhat>1.

At U0 the value of t exceeds2/5, and it increases thereafter.
Now k,k_u are positive, C_u>7 and N<13/3. The derivative is

 2N-k_u*(1+t)-k*t_u-2C_u*C_u'*t^2-2C_u^2*t*t_u
   <26/3-98/3=-24<0.                               (24)

Equations(23),(24) establish(22), including equality at the boundary.

## 7. The remaining tail comparison, entirely analytically

Fix u=U0,C=C0 and let D range in[D0,3], with m=mline(D). Put

 Astar=C0-U0-31/6=12797/16350, t=Astar/D+1/6,
 Hred(D)=Gred(U0,mline(D),C0,D).

Exact rational comparisons give0<Astar<4/5, U0>7/4,
U0^2<16/5 and C0^2<60. Differentiating, with k_m evaluated at U0,
gives

 Hred'(D)=-7N/3+(7/6)*k_m*(1+t)
                    +Astar*(k+2C0^2*t)/D^2.         (25)

Since k_m<0 and k<U0^2<16/5, while
N>(83-14D)/12, (25) is strictly less than -Fbound(D), where

 Fbound(D)=(581-98D)/36-464/(25D^2)-384/(5D^3).       (26)

For the positive term in(25), the stated estimate follows from

 (4/5)/D^2 *[16/5+120*(4/(5D)+1/6)]
       =464/(25D^2)+384/(5D^3).

The derivative of(26) is

 Fbound'(D)=-49/18+928/(25D^3)+1152/(5D^4)>0,

because D<=3 gives1152/(5D^4)>=128/45>49/18.
Also12/5<D0<5/2, so

 Fbound(D0)>28/3-29/9-50/9=5/9.

Therefore Hred'(D)<-5/9 on the entire closed interval, and its
maximum occurs at D0. This includes the diagonal extension of the
original w<=4/3,v<=10/7 slab; changing v up to the endpoint2 has not
been justified by an unsupported extrapolation of a fixed trajectory.

At the one remaining rational corner, the exact quantities are

 u=193/109, m=7/3, D=17/7, C=193/25,
 N=1342/327<411/100,
 t=67952/138975, 1+t>37/25,
 k=815039/427716>19/10,
 Ct=13114736/3474375>377/100.

Consequently

 Gred < (411/100)^2-(19/10)*(37/25)-(377/100)^2
      =-83/625.                                     (27)

All quantities bounded below in the product k(1+t) are positive.
Combining(21)--(27) proves this same strict bound for every original
word in(3) satisfying all cuts(4).

## 8. Main regional conclusion and remaining scope

For every nonnegative four-step word h=(u,w,z,v) satisfying

 sum h>=168/25, w<=4/3, 6w+7v<=18,

there is no original twenty-nonnegative-multiplier, exact-flow,
full-R5 positive semidefinite primitive certificate.

Indeed, if any cut(4) fails, the explicit one-dimensional function of
Section2 already has V_h<0. Otherwise Section5 gives the precise
legal phase of Section3. Choose rho=(1+t)/(q(1-tq)) in its explicit
static planar function(12). Then(15),(27) yield

 V_h(rho)<-83/625<0,

since(1+q)/(1-tq)>1. Its every original finite and optimum row has
been checked. Equation(2) gives the contradiction to the full original
certificate, without any assumption about a reduced cone.

This is also an empty region for the explicit expanded conjunction
N_pub AND S_scalar AND the correctly scoped nested-cut condition:
that conjunction already contains the cuts(4) and the capped member
of Section3. No implication from a smaller finite list to S_scalar is
asserted or used. The exact published inventory and its conditional
domains are neither weakened nor silently extended.

The result applies at and above the actual sufficient threshold, with
variable first step and without an upper bound on the third step.
It is a complete analytical exclusion of this stated region, not a
new independently claimed test, a perturbative surrogate for a seed,
or a global four-step exclusion. The new phase statement in Section3
only describes the displayed static family.

In particular any surviving full threshold seed must obey

 w>4/3 OR 6w+7v>18.                                 (28)

This complement must still be intersected with the full previous
inventory, including its zero/small-step reductions, every curvature
and value branch, the separate complete proportional-gradient scalar
channel, and all original coefficient and covariance constraints.
There is no exact h,Lambda,M here. No reversal or swapping of the
physical word transfers(28) without an additional proof. No negative
normalized objective error or resolution of the original asymptotic
profile Goal follows.

One analytical round remains after round39 returns within the current
completed ceiling40. All numerical, solver, machine-algebra, replay,
formal-verifier, installation, consult and subagent allowances remain
zero. The original scalar draft's audit and any corrected version must
remain separately identified; this regional proof does not validate it.

# Independent analytical audit of FINAL43a616da7da451c7

Status: UNVERIFIED research audit. No formal verifier call and no new fact.
Worker max2, one allocated round after completed baseline32, under TASK.md
and master_guidance78e668c9782fc528. This report audits the final object,
not its preliminary announcement or the author's confidence.

The public object read was
`research/shared_evidence/43a616da7da451c7.md` in the shared project,
4974 lines, SHA256
`807a671b11f0c1ac1a34af9924f23b13987ad234d62654c7cc9c58684249d88e`.
Hash matching establishes which text was read; it is not mathematical evidence.
The reproduced earlier proof51aa56ff47800613 and its appendices were read
as unverified claims within this same object.

## Verdicts and required changes

1. **Exact sum and membership in the explicitly named N_pub: survives
   independent analytic attack.** The sum is exactly168/25. All eight named
   predicate groups pass, with their conditional domains, all value and
   curvature parameters, quantitative cut slack, and ALL-prefix quantifier.
   No omitted named test was located inside this declared inventory. This is
   not a claim to enumerate every possible necessary condition. The general
   lossless domain reduction for arbitrary words is not needed to check this
   explicit member and is not fully audited here; see the coverage limits.
2. **The stated complete S_scalar nonnegativity assertion: survives
   independent analytic attack after the explicit local correction below.**
   All legal values, every curvature in[0,1], repeated positions, product-zero
   boundaries, and actual static attainment with a minimum are covered.
   However, the stronger displayed bound(I.13) is **refuted with exact data**.
   The same erroneous first-increment formula is repeated in Section5a.
3. **The actual static2D witness and full original-array exclusion: survives
   independent analytic attack.** Every original update and finite/optimum
   directed row is retained. The claimed negative quantity is primitive energy,
   not objective error. The function is globally defined and has a minimum.
4. **The physical-box results: survives independent analytic attack with the
   same low-curvature correction and the per-word quantifier stated below.**
   The scalar/unit-test equivalence holds on the stated closed box, and every
   word in the box has a static witness with primitive energy below-3/2.
   This proves no global four-step exclusion. A single fixed function common
   to the entire box has not been proved: the construction supplies F_h for
   each h, with parameters depending on that h.

This is NOT an unqualified PASS of every assertion in the long package.
The false displayed estimate must be corrected, the common-function wording
should be clarified, and the explicitly unaudited global-reduction arithmetic
must not be represented as having passed this audit. None of the four central
mathematical conclusions is refuted by the error found.

## 1. Matched accepted inputs and notation

Only accepted04219554e41d9750, proof Section10, and1252b8823bb5a290,
proof paragraphs1--2, are used as accepted premises. Their actual text was read.
The first supplies the original full-array identity

 V_h + sum_(i!=j) Lambda_ij Q_ij = <M,Gram(g0,...,g4)>,

with all twenty Lambda_ij nonnegative, exact value flow, and M PSD on all R5.
The second supplies the signed interpolation orientation and the explicit
finite-jet construction. No conclusion about existence of a threshold seed is
imported from the first fact's conditional theorem. No terminal-edge deletion
from the second fact is used here. In particular g4 remains sampled in every
energy and every interpolation test.

Throughout this report, canonical excesses are a,b,c,d and
h=(1+a,1+b,1+c,1+d). Write C=1+sum h, s=C-1,
P=2+a, N=3+a+b, Dtail=2+d, Htail=3+c+d,
t=c/Dtail and eta=b/Htail. For actual data with smoothness1,

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
 V_h=2sum_(i<4)h_i(f_i-f4)+sum_(i<4)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                                   -sC||g4||^2.

The trace pairing on the right of the full identity is nonnegative on an
actual gradient Gram if M is PSD. Thus an actual negative V_h excludes all
such arrays. This uses only the sufficient-certificate implication, not an
unproved equivalence between nonnegative energy and array feasibility.

## 2. The exact word, capacity, and denominator domains

Use the word in(I.1), equivalently Part II(6):

 C=193/25, lambda=1+sqrt(2), epsilon=1/1000,
 t0=(C-lambda)/(C+lambda), N0=C*t0,
 a=3/4, b=N0-15/4+epsilon,
 c=lambda*t0-epsilon, d=sqrt(2)-1=1/lambda.

Then N=N0+epsilon and

 5+a+b+c+d = lambda+(C+lambda)t0 = C.

Hence the four physical steps sum to C-1=168/25 exactly. This is a
symbolic perturbation of a cut equality; it is not a numerical tolerance.

The square comparisons 7/5<sqrt(2)<10/7 imply12/5<lambda<17/7.
C(C-y)/(C+y) is decreasing, so

 4<178718/44400<N0<25669/6325<65/16,
 4<N<407/100, 1/4<b<8/25<1/3.

For y in[12/5,17/7], y(C-y)/(C+y) increases: its derivative has
numerator C^2-2Cy-y^2>25/2. Its lower endpoint is1596/1265,
which exceeds5/4 by59/5060>epsilon. At the other endpoint it is below9/7
because8C<442/7. Thus

 5/4<c<9/7, 0<d<3/7,
 ab<6/25, bc<72/175<1/2, cd<27/49<1,
 abN<1221/1250<1, bN<814/625<4/3.

Every denominator in N1--N8 used for this word is positive. In particular
bt=bc/lambda<1, eta<2/27<1/13, a*eta<1, N-2>0,
Ktail=c^2*d+(c+d+2)(1-cd)>0, and all physical steps exceed1.
The proposed compact-domain inequalities hold directly for these numbers;
their general necessity for arbitrary words is not an assumption used here.

The near-saturated third cut is still strictly unsaturated:

 N*lambda-C*c=(C+lambda)*epsilon>0,
 N-C*t=epsilon*(1+C/lambda)>0.

Consequently C*a*b*c*d=ab*Ct<abN<1. The unit quadratic has strictly
positive energy Qunit=1-C^2*a^2*b^2*c^2*d^2.

## 3. Membership in each named part of N_pub

### N1: cuts, returns, clipping, and actual quadratics

All four cuts are strict. The first reduces to C>7. For the second,
Cb<8/3<P*Htail because P=11/4 and Htail>9/2. The third is the exact
epsilon identity above. For the fourth, Cd<4<C-1-d, using C>15/2,d<1/2.

The suffix condition Ccd<=N is Ct<N. The complete clipping list is
checked with its actual conditionals:

 Cb/Htail<P, Cbd<4/3<P(2+c),
 C|bc-(1+d)min(bc,1)|=Cbcd=b*Ct<bN<4/3<P.

The implication Cd<=P when bc>=1 has a false antecedent here. It is
not silently imposed without its antecedent or omitted after changing domain.
The use of the clipping elimination has its required cd<1.

Appendix A's clipping argument covers the entire alpha>0 family by its
monotone branches at b/Htail,b/(2+c),b and, only if bc>=1,
(b+1)/(1+c). The branch with final x4>1 fails both the corresponding
test and energy nonnegativity. The alpha->0 limit does not create a new
maximum. Thus the finite clipping list is not merely sampled alpha values.

All actual quadratic curvatures pass. One direct way to see this within the
present joint audit is B_full(theta)=J_theta+theta(1-theta)sum h_i*d_i,
whose second term is nonnegative and whose first is proved below, with the
corrected first increment. Independently, Part II(18) is a valid identity:
with v=h3=sqrt(2), H=h0+h1+h2 and p=p3(k),

 B_full(k)-Vaux_2(k)
 =k^3*v^2*p^2*(1-k)*[2-v+2s(1+Ck)(1+k)]/(1+kv)^2>=0.

It follows by substituting H[1+k(H+2v+1)]
=s(1+Ck)-v[1+k(v+1)], multiplying by(1+kv)^2, and using v^2=2.
It holds also when p=0, without division by p. The auxiliary positivity
needed for this second route is checked under N5.

### N2--N3: all named triangles, both terminal lifts, and lifted return

At a=3/4 the exact constants are

 kfirst=119/48, Rfirst=61/12,
 Jfirst=61/12-(119/48)eta,
 Erow=Jfirst-C^2 eta^2>4.

Indeed the two losses are below1/5 and2/5. This checks the mixed second
cut including its actual positive initial-step correction.

The first adjacent triangle is below31/16<Htail. The middle triangle
is below(1/2)(1+11/21)<1<Dtail. For the lifted middle triangle, the
left side is below35/8 while Dtail*Jfirst>48/5.

For the final triangle let S=N+c=C-lambda. Multiplication by the
positive lambda*N^2 turns its slack into

 lambda*N^2-c[N^2+c(N+C)]
 =S[lambda*S-c(S+2lambda)]=S(C+lambda)epsilon>0.

The identity d(d+2)=1 implies rtail=t^2; it is a true equality at the
chosen terminal step, not a dropped max branch. For the retained normal
lift, qpre=max(qlate,qearly)>=qlate gives

 Jpre>=N^2-kappa_pre(1+t),
 kappa_pre=(N-1)[b(N+1)-(N-1)]/b<0.

Thus Jpre>N^2>C^2*rtail. This retains the max even when its early-normal
branch is active. The independent quadratic-prefix lift satisfies

 Jquad-N^2=(1+t)[1/(a^2*b)-b*N^2]/(1+b)>0,

using abN<1. Therefore both the min(Jpre,N^2) and max(t^2,rtail)
conditions pass with their prescribed definitions.

The lifted-return left side C^2*b^2*d^2 is below16/9; its right side
Rfirst(2+c)^2-kfirst*b*(2+c) exceeds41. Its full expression, not only
the unlifted consequence, is checked. The endpoint/product consequences
listed after N3 follow from these retained conditions; they are not extra
unsupported feasibility assumptions.

### N4: every C82 parameter and its full value price

Let ell82=c(1+d)(Dtail-bc)/Dtail^2 and K=C^2(bc/Dtail)^2.
The word satisfies ell82>133/240>1/2 and K<(bN)^2<16/9.
Every legal triangle parameter r>=b,z>=0 obeys r>=1/a=4/3 and
ell82<=z^2<=H82(r)=(r+1)(ar-1). Thus the unpriced energy is
already at least16/9-K>0.

For the priced energy, use its complete minimum of two path costs and
the inequality min((r-b)(ar-1),H82(r)-z^2)<=H82(r)-z^2.
This gives the lower bound

 (1-a^2)r^2-2(1+a)a*r+1+2(1+a)
                   +[2(1+a)+1-b^2]z^2-K.

The first polynomial at a=3/4 is (7/16)(r-3)^2+9/16, and the z^2
coefficient exceeds4. Hence every legal parameter and every legal
value choice has optimized energy above

 9/16+4ell82-K>113/144.

This proves the full parameter inequality directly; the clipped stationary
formula from Appendix F is unnecessary for this word. Its path-price proof
also keeps optimum rows: repricing f0 by minus the least incoming cost
increases each incoming residual by at most its available slack and decreases
every outgoing residual. The source arcs from4 to1,2,3 have zero cost.
No unpriced-only C82 replacement is being made.

### N5: every C83/C86 curvature and repair branch

The required auxiliary is not the complete scalar-polynomial channel.
Use its own prefix polynomials p_i(k), suffix mass R, prefix mass H,
E=1+kR, and

 V_k/k=B(k)-H*F_HR(k)*p_(j+1)(k)^2,
 F_HR(k)=[1+k(H+2R+1)]/(1+kR)^2.

The full residual list in Appendix B gives beta=k^3*Tplus/(2R*E^2)
and V_k=k^3*Qcut/E^2 for R>0. The possibly positive residuals are
prefix-to-suffix rows; their largest ratio to suffix mass is attained at
the first suffix node. Thus beta=0 really makes the entire auxiliary legal.
Multiplying the exact repaired energy by positive factors gives precisely
N5(2), without dividing by beta or a prefix product.

Here the hypotheses used in the continuum sign proof are checked directly:
1<h0,h3<9/5,1<h1,h2<49/20,C<87/10 and bc<2+d. There is no reliance
on proving that arbitrary candidates reduce to this domain.

For k*h_i<=1 on the prefix, the product bound for later factors is
at most1/(1+k times their step sum). The exact inequality used to compare
each prefix summand, with r=k*h_i and t=k times the later prefix mass, is

 (1+k-r)(1+t)^2-(1+k+r+2t)(1-r)^2
 =r[2k+(1-k)r-r^2]+2(k+r-r^2)t+(1+k-r)t^2>=0.

This proves nonnegative auxiliary energy before the first reciprocal root,
including zero factors. The mixed-sign bounds from Appendix B can be checked
without any monotonicity assumption across roots. For the second cut they
give V_k/k>9/32 if h1>=h0 and >73/500 if h0>h1. For the third cut
the three exhaustive cases are k*h0<=1; k*h0>1 but k*h1<=1; and
k*h0,k*h1>1 but k*h2<=1. Respectively the bounds are

 V_k/k>239/2560, >1859/40000, >9/25-121/540>0.

To detail the decisive estimates, put m=49/20,m0=9/5. In the first
case, |p3|<=4(m-1)^3/(27m)<3/16 and B>4/5, while H<67/10,F_HR<3.
In the second, |p2|<4/45, p2^2<1/100, |p3|<29/225 with
p3^2<1/60, and B>13959/40000,H<121/20. In the third,
the two-step tail contribution is positive because
1-m(1-k)(mk-1)>=759/1600; |p3|<3364/33075<1/9 and
B>9/25,H<121/20. The second-cut estimates use F_HR<3/2,
H<17/4 or18/5, and the exact two-factor negative-lobe maximum
(x-y)^2/(4xy). These inequalities include every reciprocal boundary.

Above all reciprocal roots, define f_x(k)=x[1-k(x-1)]/(xk-1)^2.
Its derivative is x[x(x-1)k-x-1]/(xk-1)^3. The normalized second-cut
energy f_x+y[1-k(y-1)]-H*F_HR*(yk-1)^2 strictly decreases.
For the third cut the normalized energy is

 f_x/(yk-1)^2+f_y+z[1-k(z-1)]-H*F_HR*(zk-1)^2.

Its first derivative contribution is below-1; any positive f_y' is
below1; the last two derivatives are negative. The stated bounds follow
from x<m0 and y<m. Thus this normalized energy also strictly decreases.
At k=1 the two actual values for the word are

 1-C^2*a^2*eta^2>1-(6/13)^2>0,
 1-C^2*a^2*b^2*t^2>1-(abN)^2>0.

This proves positivity over both entire middle-cut curvature intervals.
Since V0j>=0 and Tplus>=0, the complete repaired tests pass, including
all branch ties and zero credit. At j=0, Qcut is affine, positive at0
and nonnegative at1 by its base cut; at j=3,R=0 the test is the true
quadratic condition already checked under N1. Any equivalent finite
C86 root test is covered by this direct continuum proof; no missing
finite-root selection is being used as a substitute.

### N6: FULL2948

Here u0=7/4,delta0=17/16,0<nu<56/39<3/2. Since cd<1 and ab<1,
Krow=u0. We have Erow>4>nu*delta0. Hence

 Krow*Erow/nu>u0*delta0,

which checks the full inequality(3) even before adding its square-root
term. Qunit>0 and nu>0 hold. The strict consequences also follow since
delta0<u0^2. No use was made of the weaker corollary in place of FULL2948.
Appendix C's incoming weights have the correct upper bound even when
1-bc is negative; for this word all these weights are nonnegative anyway.

### N7: quantitative cut slack and ALL-prefix implication

All beta_j>0 follow from the strict cuts. The useful quantitative bounds
in Part II(19) are valid: beta1>4, beta2>9/800>1/100, beta3>10.
For beta2 the numerator factors as
epsilon(C+lambda)(N*lambda+C*c)>epsilon*10*18 and its denominator
(lambda+c)^2 is below16. Thus this is a genuinely positive perturbation
of the cut boundary, not an unnoticed use of the excluded saturated word.

The last summand alone pays for N7(4):

 (h3*p3(1))^2*beta3-Qunit
 =a^2*b^2*c^2*(C-sqrt(2))^2-1>0,

because abc(C-sqrt(2))>45/32. The other summands in the square-root
sum are nonnegative.

For any actual prefix of mass Pj, convexity at both sampled endpoints
and the GD updates give |f_i-f_j|<=Pj*Gmax^2. Therefore, in every
dimension and for every legal prefix trajectory,

 V_prefix>=-(4Pj^2+Pj)*Gmax^2.

The three losses are at most2Pj^2 from values, Pj^2 from the gradient
diagonal, and Pj(Pj+1) from the terminal term. No particular prefix
trajectory or minimizer is selected. This also holds when Gmax=0.

Compare with the actual constants Rbd,Kbd,Lbd in N7, retaining all
four original beta values and the specified mstep. The cut gaps imply
Rbd>=0 and B-dcut>0. At j=0 the prefix is empty. At j=1,
Ebd>12,dcut<1/3 and2B/(B-dcut)>2 give Lbd>72, so
Rbd+Kbd*Lbd>72>14=4(7/4)^2+7/4. At j=2,
Ubd>4/25, Ebd>112/25, Lbd>168/25 and Kbd>17/2 give

 Rbd+Kbd*Lbd>1428/25>91/2>4Pj^2+Pj,

using3<Pj<13/4. At j=3,Ebd>20,dcut<1/2 give Lbd>80,
Kbd>10 and5<Pj<6, so the budget exceeds800>150>4Pj^2+Pj.
Thus N7(5) holds for ALL actual prefixes. Every concrete negative-prefix
inequality previously extracted from it follows as well. Exact saturated
prefix heredity has a false antecedent at every cut of this word.

### N8: complete retained row calibrations, caps, mixtures, and Psi

Use exactly the Appendix E definitions, with v=sqrt(2). All its relevant
domains hold: a,b,c>0,bc<1,0<v<2,ell=a^2*b*(1-bt)>0,
sqrt(2)-1<a<1,ab<1,c(v-1)<1,a*eta<1.
Put mu=1+t,beta=1+b and

 Rcut=ell*N^2+mu-C^2*a^2*b*beta*t^2,
 Dprefix=b^2*N^2-P^2.

The exact Jquad identity gives Rcut=a^2*b*beta*(Jquad-C^2*t^2)>0.
Also

 Dprefix<16/9-121/16=-833/144=-u0^2*delta0/a^2.

Thus for every nonnegative price parameter k,
eps_k=k*Rcut-Dprefix>u0^2*delta0/a^2. This already proves all the
old or sharpened positive-k ratio tests. Zero-remainder restrictions have
false antecedents. It is not necessary to select a root numerically.

For every allowed three-vector calibration, its completion of squares gives
0<=sigma<=u0^2*Lk<=u0^2/a^2. In the later original02-row calibration,
qcorr>=0 implies the same upper bound by subtracting the square in its
definition of sigma. These bounds also cover cap transitions; sigma=0
leaves only its nonnegative-remainder inequality.

For Q=Qunit>0 and Qplus=Q+u0^2*delta0>0, the unrestricted supremum
over every real price r<A0/u0, A0>0, is

 sup[r*u0*delta0-r^2*Q/(A0-r*u0)]
       =(A0/u0^2)(sqrt(Qplus)-sqrt(Q))^2<=A0*delta0.

This follows by setting y=A0-r*u0>0 and completing the square in
sqrt(Qplus*y) and A0*sqrt(Q/y). Imposing any original incoming-coefficient
cap only decreases this supremum. Consequently the strict eps_k bound
above pays every unmixed calibrated test, including negative prices.
For every lambda_mix>=0, Erow>nu*delta0 gives

 eps_k+lambda_mix*Erow
 >(u0^2/a^2+lambda_mix*nu)*delta0
 >=(sigma+lambda_mix*nu)*delta0.

This pays the entire corresponding mixture before any cap is imposed.
It uses the specified common budgets; no matching of separately chosen
original multipliers is being inferred.

For the terminal Psi, the actual original-row root obeys k>k0>0.
Its c1=beta*ell*mu*(k-k0), so the Pcost term
ell*mu*beta/c1 is exactly1/(k-k0). Thus
Gcost>=1/(k-k0). Every term in Psi is nonnegative; all its radicals are
defined. In particular nbar>=0 is exactly implied by the retained terminal
cut v<=2C/(C+1). Because Dprefix<0,

 Rcut<eps/k<eps/(k-k0)<=Psi(sqrt(eps)).

This proves the terminal test on its actual domain. The ell=0,v=0 or
zero-cap historical branches are not used for this word. The global
redundancy assertion about other words at the beginning of N8 is unnecessary
for this direct membership argument and receives no separate audit approval.

## 4. Exact scalar minimum, static attainment, and the false bound

For fixed theta in[0,1], put p0=1,p_i=product_(r<i)(1-theta*h_r),
x_i=p_i,g_i=theta*p_i and f_i^0=theta*p_i^2/2. These satisfy every
original GD update. With f_i=f_i^0+r_i and Utheta=theta(1-theta)/2,
all ordered finite residuals reduce exactly to

 Q_ij=r_i-r_j-Utheta*(p_i-p_j)^2.

Let d_i be the least sum of squared edge lengths from p4 to p_i through
the five sampled coordinates. Splitting an edge at intervening sorted
coordinates decreases its squared cost. Every path crosses all the gaps
between its endpoints. Therefore the exact distance is the sum of squared
successive distinct sorted gaps. This proof includes tied coordinates.
The path inequalities give r_i-r4>=-Utheta*d_i, and all these bounds
are simultaneously feasible because d_j<=d_i+(p_i-p_j)^2.
All h_i are nonnegative, so

 J_theta=B_theta-theta(1-theta)sum_(i<4)h_i*d_i

is the exact minimum over ALL legal values, not just selected quadratic
values. Amplitude and sign changes multiply it by the amplitude squared;
zero amplitude has zero energy.

Static attainment also survives. For the optimized values put
cjet_i=g_i*x_i-f_i and

 H_i(y)=cjet_i+x_i(y-g_i)+(y-g_i)^2/2,
 H=max_i H_i, F(x)=sup_y(xy-H(y)).

H is finite and1-strongly convex, so the maximizer is unique and depends
1-Lipschitz-continuously on x by strong subgradient monotonicity.
The maximizing inequalities prove differentiability of F with this maximizer
as derivative. H_j(g_i)-cjet_i=Q_ij<=0 and equality at j=i establish
the complete interpolation. The minimizing points are exactly

 partial H(0)=conv{(1-theta)p_i:H_i(0)=max_j H_j(0)},

a nonempty hull. Thus a minimum exists without prescribing a spurious
optimum at the base origin. No true-distance objective payoff is inferred.

The chronological-route formula(I.10) and general increment(I.11) are
correct. Appending a physical step t to mass S gives

 E_new-E_old=theta^3*t*(S+t)*p_m^2
                      [2(S+1)-theta*t*(S+t)].

**The specialization in Section3 is incorrect.** At S=0,t=u=7/4,
the first increment is

 E1=u^2*theta^3*(2-u^2*theta),

not u^2*theta^3*(2-u*theta). The latter appears again in(I.13) and
the box proof in Section5a.

An exact refutation of(I.13) uses the same word and theta=4/7=1/u.
Then p0=1 and p1=p2=p3=p4=0. All legal values obey
f1=f2=f3=f4 and theta^2/2<=f0-f4<=theta-theta^2/2.
The minimum is attained at f0=8/49 and all other values0. A concrete
convex1-smooth function attaining these values and gradients is

 F(x)=0                              for x<=3/7,
      (x-3/7)^2/2                    for3/7<=x<=1,
      (4/7)(x-1)+8/49                for x>=1.

Its derivative is continuous, nondecreasing, and1-Lipschitz; it has
minimizers. The actual first step sends1 to0, and all later gradients
vanish. Thus

 J_(4/7)=2*(7/4)*(8/49)+(7/4)(1-7/4)*(16/49)=1/7.

But(I.13) claims a lower bound4/7. This is an actual legal all-value
counterexample to that displayed estimate, not numerical evidence.

The smallest repair changes each first-step factor2-u*theta to
2-u^2*theta and replaces the sentence claiming its bracket is at least1
by 2-u^2*theta>=2-u=1/4>0. All later chronological increments in
0<theta<=4/7 remain nonnegative. In particular the third-step bracket
is at least376/343 by minimizing2(S+1)-theta*z(S+z) at
S=3,theta=4/7,z=16/7; its derivative in S is positive there.
The corrected complete low-curvature conclusion is

 J_theta>=u^2*theta^3*(2-u^2*theta)>0.

No repair submission or modification of the author's object was made.

## 5. The rest of the scalar interval and all boundaries

For4/7<=theta<=1/w, where w=h1, the direct-route bound(I.14) is exact
as a comparison path bound. With m=(u*theta-1)(1-w*theta),

 0<=m<=(u-w)^2/(4uw)<=1/35,
 |p2|,|p3|,|p4|<=1/35.

The latter bounds use0<z*theta-1<=29/35 and|1-v*theta|<1.
The only negative coefficient h_i(2-h_i) is at z=h2 and its magnitude
is at most32/49<1; u(2-u)=7/16. Therefore

 J_theta/theta^2>=7/16-169/1225>1/4.

This does not divide by any p_i and includes both signs of p4 and all
product zeros in this interval.

For1/w<theta<=1, w<v<u<z makes all step factors negative. Put
A=u*theta-1,B=w*theta-1,Z=z*theta-1,V=v*theta-1,Pprod=ABZV=p4.
Now0<Pprod<=abcd<1/C<1/7, so the direct-route term involving
p4(1-p4) is nonnegative. Define

 U=7/16,W=w(2-w)>8/9,Zc=z(z-2)<1,D=v(2-v)>0,
 L=U/Pprod^2+(W/B^2-Zc)/(Z^2*V^2)+D/V^2-s^2-2s/theta.

Then J_theta>=theta^2*Pprod^2*L. The middle fraction decreases
because its positive numerator decreases and its denominator increases;
D/V^2 decreases. The first derivative is below-49, since U>1/4,
u/A>2 and1/Pprod^2>49. The only increasing term has derivative
2s/theta^2<224/9<25. Thus L'<-24. At theta=1,
L(1)=1/(abcd)^2-C^2>0. This proves positivity on the entire last interval.

At theta=0 all coordinates agree, all gradients vanish, and all legal values
agree, so J0=0. At theta=1 the price vanishes and the unit inequality is
strict. The roots1/z,1/u,1/v,1/w lie in intervals handled without a
division by a vanishing product. Changes in the sorted coordinate order
cause no gap: the shortest-path formula is valid at ties and both route
upper bounds apply regardless of coordinate order.

Consequently S_scalar itself survives with the stated correction. It is
strict for every positive theta and zero at theta=0. This conclusion is
about the specified proportional-gradient polynomial geometry only; it
does not assert anything about every nonlinear scalar GD trajectory.

## 6. The separating static two-dimensional function

Put q=a/(2+b),t=c/(1+v),rho=(1+t)/(q(1-tq)),v=1+d.
The word has0<q<3/8,0<t<15/28,tq<1, so rho>0. Define the actual
two-coordinate samples by(I.24):

 Xbase=(N,2+b,1,-c,-t), Gbase=(1,1,1,-t,-t),
 Xaux=(1,-a,-q,cq,tq), Gaux=(1,-q,-q,tq,tq),
 X_i=(Xbase_i,sqrt(rho)*Xaux_i),
 G_i=(Gbase_i,sqrt(rho)*Gaux_i),
 F_i=<X_i,G_i>-||G_i||^2/2.

All four updates hold in both coordinates, using a=(2+b)q and
c=(1+v)t. This includes the first two different auxiliary gradients and
the original terminal gradient. No compression or selected-row relaxation
has been introduced.

For saturated values, Q_ij=(g_i-g_j)(x_j-g_j) in each coordinate.
The base target normals are(N-1,1+b,0,-vt,0), and its gradient at each
nonzero-normal target is the appropriate endpoint of[-t,1]; hence all
base residuals are nonpositive. The auxiliary target normals are
(0,-(1+b)q,0,vtq,0). At target1 every source gradient is at least-q,
so every residual is nonpositive. At target3 only source0 has a positive
residual, namely(1-tq)vtq. Its base residual is-(1+t)vt. Weighting by
rho cancels this pair exactly. At all remaining targets the normals are
zero. This checks every finite directed pair, including all pairs with4.

Every residual into the zero optimum is zero. In the reverse direction
Q_(star,i)=-<G_i,X_i-G_i>. The nonzero base products areN-1,1+b,vt^2,
and the auxiliary products are(1+b)q^2 and vt^2q^2. They are nonnegative,
so every optimum row holds too.

Let K=conv{G0,G1,G3}. Since G2=G1 and G4=G3=-tG1, zero belongs
to K. Define one static function for this word by

 F(X)=max_(G in K)(<X,G>-||G||^2/2).

Completing the square identifies its unique maximizing gradient as the
projection onto K. The checked normal inequalities give that gradient G_i
and the prescribed value at every X_i. Adding two projection inequalities
gives the1-Lipschitz bound; maximizing inequalities give differentiability.
The function is convex and finite, and F>=0=F(0), so a minimum exists.
Neither uniqueness of the minimizer nor any paid radius assertion is used.

Saturated values telescope, and X4=G4, giving

 V_h=||X0||^2-C^2||G4||^2
    =N^2-C^2t^2+rho*(1-C^2t^2q^2).

Here Ct>39/10,q>9/28, so Ctq>351/280>5/4 and the second parenthesis
is below-9/16. Also rho>1/q>8/3. The base term is strictly positive
but below13/300<1/20 by the exact cut-gap factorization. Hence

 V_h<1/20-(8/3)(9/16)=-29/20<0.

The accepted full identity makes this incompatible with every original
twenty-sign, exact-flow, full-R5 array. No rank, support, symmetry,
step ordering, or certificate-reversal claim is needed. This does not
make F(X4)-min F negative: the negative object is the primitive energy.

## 7. Uniform physical-box claims and their precise quantifiers

Let Bbox be the closed set

 h=(7/4,w,z,v),
 5/4<=w<=4/3,9/4<=z<=16/7,7/5<=v<=10/7.

All scalar interval estimates in Sections4--5 above remain valid uniformly
there, after correcting the first increment. In the high interval assume
the unit test C*Pbox<=1, Pbox=(3/4)(w-1)(z-1)(v-1)>0.
Then Pprod<=Pbox<=1/C<1/7, L'<-24 and L(1)>=0. Thus all smaller
positive curvatures give positive energy and theta=1 gives nonnegative
energy. Conversely, at theta=1 the exact value is1-C^2*Pbox^2, so
S_scalar implies the unit test. This proves both directions throughout the
closed box, including equality in the unit test and all physical boundaries.

For the nested witness, take arbitrary h in this box and set
a=3/4,b=w-1,c=z-1,d=v-1 with its actual q,t,rho. The estimates
do not assume the unit test, N_pub, or threshold capacity. Uniformly,

 N<=49/12, C>=153/20,
 35/68<=t<=15/28,9/28<=q<=1/3,tq<=5/28,
 Ct>=63/16,Ctq>=81/64.

They imply

 rho>(1+t)/q>=309/68>9/2,
 1-C^2t^2q^2<=-2465/4096<-3/5,
 N^2-C^2t^2<6/5,

the last comparison following from
(49/12)^2-(63/16)^2=2695/2304<6/5. All original-row checks in Section6
used only these positive excesses and tq<1, not the equation v=sqrt(2).
Thus for each h in the closed box the actual static witness has

 V_h<6/5-(9/2)(3/5)=-3/2.

The quantifier proved is

 for every h in Bbox, there exists the displayed static F_h and its
 full original trajectory with V_h<-3/2.

The hull K and its q,t,rho generally vary with h. Phrases such as
"the SAME nested static function" should mean the same construction; they
do not establish existence of one fixed function independent of h. The
smallest wording repair is "the same explicit family of word-dependent
static functions". A literal common-function assertion has incomplete checks.

Every word in the box therefore lacks a full primitive array. Its threshold
portion is excluded too, and the displayed exact word proves that the OLD
N_pub AND S_scalar intersection there is nonempty. There is no reduction of
all possible four-step seeds to this box, and no global no-seed theorem.

## 8. Coverage limits and handoff

The complete final public object was read, including Appendices A--F.
The core membership, scalar comparison, original witness and uniform box
arguments have been checked as detailed above. Two kinds of claims receive
no blanket approval from this audit:

* I did not independently recompute the entire large Bernstein coefficient
  list in Appendix A Section7 or every large rational numerator used in
  its global compact-domain reductions (Sections9,12,13). Those arguments
  concern arbitrary candidate words. They are not needed for membership
  of this explicit word: every domain hypothesis actually used above was
  established directly from its exact definition. No lossless reduction
  or global four-step exclusion is granted on the basis of this report.
* The broad redundancy assertion for other words at the start of N8,
  sharpness of its auxiliary relaxations, and claims about omitted historical
  sections are not necessary for the direct universal-parameter check here.
  The report certifies no theorem about those additional words or relaxations.

The label N_pub is interpreted as the explicit N1--N8 conjunction in
Part II Section1, with Appendix E's displayed families on their stated
domains. It does not mean every necessary test ever conceivable or every
unlisted research branch. The later nested-cut test is demonstrably not in
that old conjunction. The two scoped old word/prism exclusions listed in
N8 have hypotheses not satisfied by this word; its first physical step is
7/4 and second excess is below1/3. No saturated-cut implication is triggered.

The key required mathematical edit is localized to Part I Section3,
equation(I.13), and the repeated first-increment line in Section5a.
General formula(I.11) already has the correct extra factor of u. The explicit
counterexample to the printed lower bound was also published separately as
f0730b188bcec643. This clean report supplies the correct piecewise function
and derivation without relying on any exploratory wording in that early note.

Memory and literature retrieval were used only for context. Matlas recovered
a matching standard finite-interpolation statement and web search identified
arXiv1502.05666. No external result was imported as a new proof premise;
the two accepted project facts and the calculations above suffice.

Resource use in this round: zero numerical evaluations, solver calls,
machine-algebra jobs, replay runs, formal submissions, installations,
consult calls, or subagents. Text reads, an operational hash, and text
persistence were used. An initial worker-relative mirror path returned
"No such file or directory"; the explicitly named shared-project mirror
was then read successfully. The author's candidate was not changed.

This completes the single allocated audit round (completed33, attempted
ceiling34 including the historical interruption), without renewing any
round or resource. This is a mathematical handoff, not a claim to have
inspected the external loop's termination. Original exact-profile Goal and
W=2R remain unchanged and OPEN. No fact_submit, finalization, or publication
outside the project stores was performed.

# Quantitative positive-gap constraints from the original three-vector energy

Status: complete joint analytical proof DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. This is max4
round31, the first of the two rounds authorized after completed30 by
TASK.md and master_guidance6e0a18d700dcd62f. No numerical evaluation,
solver, replay, installation, subordinate agent, consult or fact_submit
is used. The sole accepted mathematical input is the original full
primitive identity in fact04219554e41d9750, Section10. All additional
identities and optimization arguments used in this draft are proved here.

This draft retains the THREE-vector energy discarded by the earlier
two-energy optimization. It gives an exact positive improvement of that
particular gap bound, and combines it with the actual quadratic column
in the SAME original matrix. All twenty coefficient signs are retained
in the hypotheses. It does NOT show that the new excluded strip contains
a word passing the complete older scalar/triangle/curvature system. It
does NOT solve the remaining full-column/sign completion problem. The
original threshold seed and original exact-profile question remain open.

## 1. Original matrix, notation and exact identities

Let

    h=(1+a,1+b,1+c,v), a,b,c>0, v>=0, bc<=1,
    u=1+a, beta=1+b, w=1+c,
    A=2+a, N=3+a+b, H=2+a+b, H0=1+a+b,
    C=1+sum h=4+a+b+c+v,
    t=c/(1+v), mu=1+t, gamma=w/mu,
    ell=a^2*b*(1-b*t), K=b^2*gamma^2,
    Rcut=ell*N^2+mu-C^2*a^2*b*beta*t^2,
    Dprefix=N^2*b^2-A^2,
    Gap=K*Rcut-ell*Dprefix,
    z=a*b*(w-mu)=a*b*v*t.

Here ell>=0 and gamma>=1. In this stated domain ell=0 if and only if
v=0 and bc=1. Every formula below that divides by ell is explicitly
restricted to ell>0. The case ell=0 is handled separately in Section6.

Write P_i=sum_(j<i)h_j, s=P_4=C-1, and let e_i be the five original
standard coordinate columns, indexed0 through4, and e=sum e_i. Put

    T_ii=1, T_ij=h_j if j<i, T_ij=0 if j>i,
    omega=(-2h0,-2h1,-2h2,-2h3,2s)^T,
    Dmat=diag(h0(2-h0),h1(2-h1),h2(2-h2),h3(2-h3),-s(s+2)),
    L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda, M=Dmat+(LT+T^T L^T)/2.

The hypotheses are the original twenty signs Lambda_ij>=0 for i!=j,
zero diagonal, L^T e=omega, and M>=0 on ALL of R^5. No coordinate,
multiplier, rank or support is removed. In particular v=0 does not
authorize identification of the formal gradients at indices3 and4.

The accepted polynomial identity is

    V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij=sum_(i,j)M_ij<g_i,g_j>,
    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
    V_h=2sum_(i<4)h_i(f_i-f4)+sum_(i<4)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
                                                 -s(s+1)||g4||^2.       (1)

It is valid on formal data following x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i*g_i; formal
substitutions below are not asserted to be actual trajectories unless
their interpolation inequalities are checked.

The flow implies e^T M e=0: Te=e+P,
e^T Dmat e=-s^2-sum h_i^2, and
omega^T(e+P)=s^2+sum h_i^2. Nonnegativity of
(e+r*q)^T M(e+r*q) for both signs of the real number r then gives Me=0.
The same argument gives

    |x^T M y|^2 <= (x^T M x)(y^T M y),
    x^T M x=0 implies Mx=0.                           (2)

Use the shorthand <x,y>_M=x^T M y and ||x||_M^2=<x,x>_M. Set

    E=e0, J=e0+e1, B=e3+e4,
    S=||J||_M^2, Tcut=||B||_M^2, r=<J,B>_M,
    B0=Lambda30+Lambda40, B1=Lambda31+Lambda41,
    Avec=u*E-a*beta*J-a*b*mu*B,
    c03=a*b*u*mu*v*t=u*mu*z,
    c23=a^2*b*beta*mu*v*t.

The next three identities hold for every array satisfying the original
flow and Me=0:

    Rcut=ell*mu^2*Tcut+mu*||Avec||_M^2
          +ell*mu*(H*B0+beta*B1)+c03*Lambda03+c23*Lambda23,              (3)

    Dprefix=beta*((b*w*H0-u)*B0+b^2*w*B1-2*b*w*r-beta*S
                    -u*Lambda20-b*v*c*(Lambda04+Lambda14)),            (4)

    2<E,B>_M=a*B0-Lambda03-(1+v)*Lambda04.                             (5)

Here are full derivations. For (3), take saturated values
f_i=x_i*g_i-g_i^2/2 in the two scalar arrays

    x_base=(N,2+b,1,-c,-t), g_base=(1,1,1,-t,-t),
    x_aux =(1,-a,ab,-abc,-abt), g_aux=(1,-a,ab,-abt,-abt).

Both follow the original h. Their normals x_i-g_i are respectively
(H,beta,0,-vt,0) and (0,0,0,-abvt,0). For saturated values
Q_ij=(g_i-g_j)(x_j-g_j). In the weighted sum ell times the base array
plus mu times the auxiliary array, the complete nonzero residual list is

    Q30=Q40=-ell*mu*H, Q31=Q41=-ell*mu*beta,
    Q03=-c03, Q23=-c23.

All other ordered residuals vanish. In particular Q13 cancels because
ell=a^2*b*(1-bt). At Q03 use
ell+ab*(1+abt)=ab*u, and at Q23 use
ell+a^2*b^2*mu=a^2*b*beta. The position/gradient square telescope in
(1) for saturated values is V_h=||x0||^2-||x4+s*g4||^2. Since x4=g4,
the weighted energy is Rcut. Me=0 gives g_base=-mu*B+e and
g_aux=Avec+ab*e, proving (3). No interpolation inequality is omitted.

For (4), start the scalar two-step cut at x0=A with gradients
(1,1,-b), then extend it FORMALLY with constant gradient -b through
the last two steps and affine function values. The full energy is
A^2-N^2*b^2=-Dprefix. The gradient column is beta*J-b*e.
The only negative residuals are Q20=Q30=Q40=-u*beta. The positive
ones are Q03=Q13=w*b*beta and Q04=Q14=(w+v)*b*beta.
The other ordered residuals vanish. Thus (1) gives

 Dprefix=b*beta*(w*(Lambda03+Lambda13)+(w+v)*(Lambda04+Lambda14))
                  -beta^2*S-u*beta*(Lambda20+B0).

The two original cross rows, obtained by multiplying the displayed T,
are (5) and

    2<e1,B>_M=beta*B0+b*B1-Lambda13-(1+v)*Lambda14.

Their sum eliminates Lambda03+Lambda13+(1+v)(Lambda04+Lambda14).
Because (w+v)-w(1+v)=-vc, the result is exactly (4).
These identities use the original rows; no reversal is invoked.

Finally define the original unit-quadratic column and its energy by

    p=(1,-a,ab,-abc,abc*(v-1))^T,
    Q=1-C^2*a^2*b^2*c^2*(v-1)^2,
    m=||E||_M^2, delta=u^2-2,
    W0=1+bc*(v-1).

We have Tp=e and Le=0. Telescoping
h_i(2-h_i)p_i^2=p_i^2-p_(i+1)^2 gives

    ||p||_M^2=Q>=0,
    2<E,p>_M=u*(m-delta)
                +a^2*(beta*Lambda20+(1-bc)*Lambda30+W0*Lambda40).     (6)

For completeness, put O0=sum_(j>0)Lambda0j and
I0=Lambda20+Lambda30+Lambda40. The diagonal gives
m=1-a^2-a*O0. Node0 flow gives Lambda10=O0+2u-I0.
Using Te0=u*e-a*e0 in Mp=Dmat*p+T^T L^T p/2 yields
2<E,p>_M=2(1-a^2)-a*p^T L e0. Substitute

 p^T L e0=u*O0+2a*u
             -a*(beta*Lambda20+(1-bc)*Lambda30+W0*Lambda40).

The remaining constant is -u*delta, proving (6). On bc<=1,v>=0,
0<=1-bc<=W0. Consequently every coefficient in the incoming
combination in (6) is nonnegative. This sign assertion includes v<1.

## 2. A family of exact three-vector positive-gap remainders

For ell>0 define

    k0=b^2*w/(ell*mu)=b^2*gamma/ell,
    kold=K/ell,
    U=2*beta*z/(a*u).

Choose a positive number k and a real number theta satisfying

    k>=k0,
    0<=theta<=min(U,2*k*mu*z),
    F(k,theta):=b^2*w^2-z*theta
                  +(a^2+1/(k*mu))*theta^2/4-k*ell*mu^2 <=0.          (7)

Put

    Btheta=b*w-a*theta/2,
    Atheta=Avec-theta*B/(2*k*mu),
    cB=k*ell*mu*H-beta*(b*w*H0-u)+a*u*theta/2,
    c1=beta*(k*ell*mu-b^2*w),
    c04=beta*b*v*c-u*(1+v)*theta/2,
    c03new=k*c03-u*theta/2.

There is the EXACT full-array identity

 k*Rcut-Dprefix
   =||beta*J+Btheta*B||_M^2+k*mu*||Atheta||_M^2-F(k,theta)*Tcut
      +cB*B0+c1*B1+beta*u*Lambda20
      +c04*Lambda04+beta*b*v*c*Lambda14
      +c03new*Lambda03+k*c23*Lambda23.                              (8)

All displayed coefficients on the right are nonnegative. For cB,
at k=k0,theta=0 its value is

    b^2*w*H-beta*(b*w*H0-u)=u*(1-bc)>=0;

increasing k or theta adds nonnegative terms. The other coefficients
have exactly the signs in (7).

To check (8), expand its two squares. Their J-B cross coefficient is
2*beta*b*w because -a*theta*beta from the first square cancels
+a*theta*beta from -theta<Avec,B>_M in the second. Their E-B
cross contribution is -u*theta<E,B>_M. The Tcut coefficient is

    (b*w-a*theta/2)^2+a*b*mu*theta+theta^2/(4*k*mu)
                      =k*ell*mu^2+F(k,theta).

Now use (5) in the remaining u*theta<E,B>_M. Its contributions are
+a*u*theta*B0/2,-u*theta*Lambda03/2, and
-u*(1+v)*theta*Lambda04/2. Combining (3) and (4) gives precisely
every coefficient in (8). This proof divides by no matrix energy.

In particular, every full original certificate satisfies

                         Dprefix<=k*Rcut.                            (9)

## 3. The optimal constant in this three-vector relaxation

The minimizer of F(k,theta) before the upper bounds in (7) is

    theta_free(k)=2*z/(a^2+1/(k*mu)).

It always obeys theta_free<=2*k*mu*z. Thus the optimum allowed value
is

    theta_opt(k)=min(theta_free(k),U).                                (10)

For v>0 one has z>0 and k0<kold. At k=k0, even the unrestricted
minimum is strictly positive. Indeed, if Hk=a^2*k0*mu,

    min_theta F(k0,theta)/mu^2
      =b^2*(gamma-1)*(gamma+Hk)/(1+Hk)>0.                            (11)

At k=kold, its allowed minimum is strictly negative: choose any
positive theta smaller than both upper bounds and sufficiently close
to zero, and the constant term vanishes while -z*theta dominates the
positive quadratic term. For each fixed theta,

    partial F/partial k=-ell*mu^2-theta^2/(4*k^2*mu)<0.

Also (10) minimizes over the fixed interval [0,U], since the other
bound is automatic at that minimizer. Thus this minimum is continuous
and strictly decreasing in k. It follows that there is a unique

    kstar in (k0,kold), F(kstar,theta_opt(kstar))=0.                   (12)

This is an exact algebraic number, without a numerical root search.
Here are explicit branches. Define

    Bint=a^2*b^2*mu*(2*gamma-1)-ell,
    kint=(Bint+sqrt(Bint^2+4*a^2*ell*mu*b^2*gamma^2))
                                                       /(2*a^2*ell*mu).

If theta_free(kint)<=U, then kstar=kint. Otherwise define

    V0=b^2*w^2-z*U+a^2*U^2/4,
    V1=U^2/(4*mu),
    kbound=(V0+sqrt(V0^2+4*ell*mu^2*V1))/(2*ell*mu^2).

Then kstar=kbound. These are obtained by multiplying respectively
F(k,theta_free(k))=0 and F(k,U)=0 by their positive denominators.
If the free root violates the cap, the increasing theta_free remains
above U at every larger k; the boundary root is therefore the correct
one. Equality of the branch conditions gives the same root.

At v=0 with ell>0, z=U=0, gamma=1 and k0=kold=b^2/ell. Set
kstar=kold and theta_opt=0; (7)--(9) still hold. No strict gain is
claimed on this boundary.

For v>0,ell>0 the new bound is strictly stronger than the old
two-energy gap bound Dprefix<=kold*Rcut whenever Dprefix>0:

    Rcut>=Dprefix/kstar,
    Gap>= (K/kstar-ell)*Dprefix >0.                                 (13)

Thus it excludes an explicit POSITIVE gap strip, not just Gap=0.
This comparison is ONLY with the previous nested two-energy bound.
It is not a comparison with the entire older necessary system.

The constant kstar is sharp for the following explicitly delimited
relaxation: retain arbitrary PSD Gram data for (E,J,B), (3)--(5),
and nonnegative quantities B0,B1,Lambda20,Lambda03,Lambda04,
Lambda14,Lambda23, but impose no remaining original rows or quadratic
column. This sharpness does not assert a full certificate.

To prove it, work in a one-dimensional Gram space with B=1 and set
Tcut=1, B0=B1=Lambda20=Lambda03=Lambda14=Lambda23=0. Let
k=kstar,theta=theta_opt(kstar), x=b*w-a*theta/2 and J=-x/beta.
If the free branch applies, set E=0,Lambda04=0. The equation
theta=2*k*mu*(a*x-a*b*mu) follows from theta=theta_free.
Consequently both squares in (8) vanish and (5) holds.

On the capped branch put

    q04=2*(a*x-a*b*mu-theta/(2*k*mu))/(u*(1+v)),
    Lambda04=q04, E=-(1+v)*q04/2.

The condition theta=U<=theta_free implies q04>=0. Again both squares
and (5) hold, while c04=0. Define Rcut and Dprefix in this RELAXATION
by (3) and (4), rather than by the step-only values. Then Rcut>0
because ell*mu^2*Tcut>0, and Dprefix=kstar*Rcut. This proves exactly
the stated sharpness and explicitly identifies its missing matching
conditions: its energies have not been equated with the actual
step-only Rcut,Dprefix, and its remaining original array is absent.

## 4. Coupling to the actual quadratic column

For any choice (k,theta) satisfying (7), define

    rho=-F(k,theta)>=0,
    Lk=k*mu/(1+a^2*k*mu)>0,
    Zk=z-(a^2+1/(k*mu))*theta/2.

The three matrix-energy terms in (8) dominate sigma*m, where

    sigma=u^2*Lk*rho/(rho+Lk*Zk^2), if rho+Lk*Zk^2>0,
    sigma=u^2*Lk,                   if rho=Zk=0.                    (14)

For the proof let X=beta*J+Btheta*B, Y=Atheta. Then
a*X+Y=u*E+Zk*B. Completing the square in X gives

    ||X||_M^2+k*mu*||Y||_M^2
                        >=Lk*||u*E+Zk*B||_M^2.

If rho+Lk*Zk^2>0, complete the square in B after adding rho||B||_M^2.
The remaining E coefficient is exactly (14). If rho=Zk=0, the
previous display is Lk*u^2*m directly. This covers singular Gram
matrices; no inverse of their entries is used. In particular sigma=0
at the strictly capped kstar branch, but sigma>0 for every k>kstar
with theta=theta_opt(k). On the free kstar branch sigma>0 already.

Combining (8) with (14) leaves the common ORIGINAL coefficient budget

    k*Rcut-Dprefix >= sigma*m+cB*(Lambda30+Lambda40)
                                            +beta*u*Lambda20.       (15)

Assume sigma>0 and define

    xi_cap=min(u/a^2, cB/(a^2*W0)).                                 (16)

If W0=0, omit the second argument of this minimum; its corresponding
incoming coefficients in (6) are both zero. This convention is used
only when that denominator is zero. The derivation itself is the
division-free condition xi*a^2*W0<=cB.

For any real xi satisfying xi<=xi_cap and xi<sigma/u, put

    Zeta=xi^2/(sigma-xi*u)>=0.

The following is a further necessary inequality of the FULL original
cone:

    k*Rcut-Dprefix >= xi*u*delta-Zeta*Q.                             (17)

To check it, subtract xi*u*delta using (6). In (15), the remaining
incoming Lambda20 coefficient is beta*u-xi*a^2*beta>=0. The two
B0 coefficients are at least cB-xi*a^2*W0>=0 if xi>=0; if xi<0
they only increase. The remaining matrix terms after adding Zeta*Q
are

    (sigma-xi*u)*||E||_M^2+2*xi<E,p>_M+Zeta*||p||_M^2
         = ||sqrt(sigma-xi*u)*E
                          +xi*p/sqrt(sigma-xi*u)||_M^2 >=0.

This proof couples the energies to the ACTUAL p in the ACTUAL M.
It is a sufficient full-space separator, not an assumption that
independently selected scalar energies possess a PSD completion.
Negative xi are intentionally allowed. They expose the opposite
first-step regime and the quadratic zero boundary.

For clarity the supremum of the right side of (17) is explicit.
Write Qplus=Q+u^2*delta. If Qplus<0, letting xi tend to -infinity
in (17) is a contradiction. Thus Qplus>=0 is necessary, in addition
to Q>=0. The exact factorization is

    Qplus=a^2*(A^2-C^2*b^2*c^2*(v-1)^2).

Thus this particular feasibility prerequisite recovers the existing
scalar-return condition Cbc|v-1|<=A; it is not counted as a new
independent obstruction. Suppose Q,Qplus>=0 and xi_cap>0.

If Q>0 and Qplus>0, set

    xi_free=(sigma/u)*(1-sqrt(Q/Qplus)).

When xi_free<=xi_cap the optimized bound is

    Bcal=(sigma/u^2)*(sqrt(Qplus)-sqrt(Q))^2.                         (18)

Otherwise xi_cap<sigma/u and the bound is

    Bcal=xi_cap*u*delta-xi_cap^2*Q/(sigma-xi_cap*u).                 (19)

If Q>0,Qplus=0, the supremum is sigma*Q/u^2, obtained as
xi tends to -infinity. If Q=0, Qplus>=0 means delta>=0, and the
supremum is

    Bcal=u*delta*min(xi_cap,sigma/u).                                (20)

For delta=Q=0 this is zero. These are limits of necessary inequalities
for every finite xi, so nonattainment of a supremum causes no problem.

The optimization follows by putting y=sigma-xi*u>0 in (17):

 xi*u*delta-xi^2*Q/(sigma-xi*u)
   =sigma*delta+2*sigma*Q/u^2
         -(delta+Q/u^2)*y-Q*sigma^2/(u^2*y).

The unconstrained maximum is (18), at
y=sigma*sqrt(Q/Qplus); clipping xi above by xi_cap gives (19).
The displayed boundary formulas follow from the same expression.
The function is increasing up to its unconstrained maximum, so the
clipped value is nonnegative. In the present ell>0 domain cB>0 for
the optimizing choices used above: if v=0 then bc<1, and if v>0
the choice theta_opt>0. Hence xi_cap>0 there. Other allowed parameter
choices with cB=0 are covered by (17) without the positive-cap claim.

For every such choice with sigma>0, the final explicit test is

    Q>=0, Qplus>=0, k*Rcut-Dprefix>=Bcal.                            (21)

When v>0, Dprefix>0 and kstar<k<kold, it gives the positive-gap bound

    Gap >= (K/k-ell)*Dprefix+(K/k)*Bcal.                             (22)

The second term is strictly positive when delta!=0, provided the
displayed feasibility conditions hold. At delta=0 it is zero; no
uniform margin is inferred across that boundary.

### 4.1. Actual full quadratic-column matching on the sharp positive-gap boundary

This concerns Dprefix=kstar*Rcut>0 with v>0. It is a POSITIVE-gap
boundary, because kstar<kold and hence
Gap=(K-ell*kstar)*Rcut>0. It is not the old Gap=0 face.

Put k=kstar,theta=theta_opt(kstar), and retain Zk,Btheta from above.
Define

    rcoef=ell*mu^2+theta^2/(4*k^2*mu)>0,
    Tstar=Rcut/rcoef>0,
    Xcal=Zk^2*Tstar.

Every full original certificate on this boundary necessarily has

    B0=B1=Lambda20=Lambda03=Lambda14=Lambda23=0,
    M*(beta*J+Btheta*B)=0, M*Atheta=0,
    M*E=-(Zk/u)*M*B, M*J=-(Btheta/beta)*M*B,
    Tcut=Tstar, m=Xcal/u^2,
    Lambda04=2*Zk*Tstar/(u*(1+v)).                                  (B1)

Indeed cB,c1,beta*u,beta*b*v*c,c03new,k*c23 in (8) are all strictly
positive here. The only coefficient that may vanish is c04. For
c03new use theta<=theta_free<2*k*mu*z. With F=0, the equality
kRcut-Dprefix=0 makes both PSD energies and all positive costs vanish.
The resulting zero columns give the two linear relations in (B1).
Equation (3) then gives Rcut=rcoef*Tcut, and (5) determines Lambda04.
No division by Q or by an unknown matrix energy was made; Tstar>0
follows from the stated Dprefix>0.

On the free branch Zk=0, (B1) gives ME=0 and Lambda04=0. Equation(6)
now forces delta=0, so a=sqrt(2)-1. Thus arbitrary scalar choices of
a on the free relaxation equality ray cannot be completed to a full
array, irrespective of how the remaining covariance is chosen.

On the strictly capped branch Zk>0, the ENTIRE quadratic cross column
in the basis of Section5 is fixed by (B1) and (6):

    qB:=<B,p>_M=(u^2*delta-Xcal)/(2*Zk),
    <E,p>_M=-(Zk/u)*qB,
    <J,p>_M=-(Btheta/beta)*qB,
    ||p||_M^2=Q.                                                   (B2)

Consequently full PSD on this boundary requires the STEP-ONLY test

    Q>=0, (u^2*delta-Xcal)^2<=4*Xcal*Q.                             (B3)

Equivalently Qplus>=0 and

    (sqrt(Qplus)-sqrt(Q))^2 <= Xcal
                                   <= (sqrt(Qplus)+sqrt(Q))^2.     (B4)

This includes Q=0: it requires Xcal=u^2*delta, and every quadratic
cross entry in (B2) is zero. Since Xcal>0 in this capped case, delta
must then be positive. The UPPER bound in (B4) would be lost by only
optimizing a lower first-row energy budget such as (18).

For the equivalence, (B3) is the quadratic inequality
Xcal^2-2*(u^2*delta+2Q)*Xcal+u^4*delta^2<=0. Its roots are exactly
the squares in (B4); they are real precisely when Qplus>=0 (with
Q=0 checked directly). In the present capped case Xcal>0, so the
direct inequality also excludes Qplus<0.

More precisely, ONCE an original array has the flow, constant kernel,
and zero-column relations (B1), its entire five-gradient matrix is
PSD if and only if the two-by-two Gram matrix on (B,p) is PSD. The
columns e,E,J,B,p form a basis for v>0, and (B1) expresses all M
columns except B,p as their stated multiples or zero. Thus (B3)
is the full PSD condition for such a matched boundary array, rather
than merely an arbitrary principal submatrix test. It does not
construct that array. All remaining original entries, their exact
flow/kernel equations and their twenty signs still need simultaneous
matching. In the free branch, the E row is zero and cannot determine
qB; that covariance remains free subject to the actual remaining rows.

This supplies an explicit failure condition for independent scalar
relaxation equality data, but no high-capacity word is asserted to
meet the boundary equation, the complete older tests, or all these
remaining coefficient conditions.

## 5. Relation to the earlier first-row budget and the full column

The earlier lifted first-cut identity can be retained simultaneously.
Its exact accounting, reproduced below, uses

    eta=b/(1+w+v), nu=(1+eta)*(1-a*eta)/a,
    Erow=a+3+1/a-(u*delta/a)*eta-C^2*eta^2,
    Erow=nu*(m+a*(Lambda20+B0))+(1+eta)^2*M11
                      +(1+eta)*eta*((w+v)*Lambda12+v*Lambda13).       (23)

It applies when 0<a<1 and a*eta<1. For a full array, a<1 follows
from m=1-a^2-a*O0 and the first row moment
sum_(j>0)P_j*Lambda0j=2u>0. Indeed the index0 entry of
2Me=2Dmat*e+L*(e+P)+T^T*omega is
2(1-a^2)-sum_(j>0)P_j*Lambda0j+2au=0, proving this moment.
For (23) the condition a*eta<1 is retained as an explicit hypothesis;
it is not required for Sections1--4.

Here is a direct check of (23). Let Uphys,Vphys be orthonormal and
gfirst=-eta*Uphys+sqrt(nu)*Vphys. Use actual steps and saturated values
on the arrays

    X=(Uphys+u*gfirst,Uphys,-b*Uphys,-(1+v)*eta*Uphys,-eta*Uphys),
    G=(gfirst,Uphys,-eta*Uphys,-eta*Uphys,-eta*Uphys).

The normals at targets1 and4 are zero. At0 the incoming residual
from1 vanishes and those from2,3,4 equal -a*nu. At2 the only nonzero
residual is Q12=-(1+eta)*(b-eta). At3 the only nonzero residual is
Q13=-(1+eta)*v*eta. These follow from
||gfirst||^2=[1+(1-a)*eta]/a and
<Uphys,gfirst>+a||gfirst||^2=1-a*eta. All other finite residuals
vanish. The optimum comparisons are nonpositive under a*eta<1.
The saturated telescope gives Erow; the two gradient columns are
nu^(1/2)*e0 and (1+eta)*e1-eta*e. Substituting all costs into (1)
gives (23), since b-eta=(w+v)*eta.

The quantitative2948 constraint is recovered on its original domain,
so it remains simultaneous with the new test. Put

    Wrow=max(beta,W0), Krow=max(u,a*Wrow).

By (23), m+a*(Lambda20+B0)<=Erow/nu. Equation (6) and (2) give

    u*delta<=Krow*Erow/nu+2*sqrt(Q*Erow/nu).                        (26)

Indeed the incoming weighted sum in (6) is at most
Wrow*(Lambda20+B0), and m<=Erow/nu. For delta>0, rationalizing
the positive quadratic root in sqrt(Erow/nu) yields

    Erow>=nu*(u*delta/(sqrt(Q+Krow*u*delta)+sqrt(Q)))^2>0.           (27)

On the original additional domain v>1, ab<=1, c*(v-1)<=1, we have
Krow=u and Q<1. If Erow/nu<=delta^2/u^2, the right side of (26)
is strictly below delta*(delta+2)/u=u*delta, a contradiction. Hence

    Erow>nu*delta^2/u^2,
    b<(a+3)*(C-a-2)/((a+1)*(C+a+2)).                              (28)

For the equivalence in (28), use the exact factorization

 u^2*(Erow-nu*delta^2/u^2)
    =((a+3)-(delta+u*C)*eta)*((a+3)+(u*C-delta)*eta).

The second factor is positive since C>A and u*A>delta. Substituting
eta=b/(C-A-b) into the positive first factor gives the stated bound.
Thus no part of the2948 restriction is silently weakened or assumed
outside its hypotheses. At Q=0, (26)--(27) remain undivided.

The two budgets (15) and (23) constrain the same m,Lambda20,B0.
One cannot minimize each with different incoming arrays and call the
answers a simultaneous certificate. The new inequality (21) is a
necessary projection of their shared full matrix, not a sufficiency
statement, and does not replace the older row bound2948.

For v>0 the columns e,E,J,B,p are a basis: the first four have equal
entries at indices3 and4, whereas p3-p4=-abc*v!=0. In this basis full
PSD is equivalent to

    [[m,       <E,J>_M, <E,B>_M, <E,p>_M],
     [<E,J>_M, S,       r,       <J,p>_M],
     [<E,B>_M, r,       Tcut,    <B,p>_M],
     [<E,p>_M, <J,p>_M, <B,p>_M, Q       ]] >=0.                    (24)

Condition (17) uses the actual column p but prices only its E cross
entry explicitly. It has NOT determined the two other entries
<J,p>_M,<B,p>_M. Their being compatible with (24), the fixed original
flow, and every one of the twenty signs is the unresolved simultaneous
obstruction. The previously completed covariance-fiber decision solves
one direction only after all of its other coordinates have been
matched. No such high-capacity match is asserted here.

When Q=0, PSD in (24) requires the WHOLE last column to vanish, not
only the E entry in (6). When a three-vector energy is zero, its
whole original M column vanishes by (2). These zero conditions must
not be replaced by independent scalar nonnegativity tests.

## 6. Boundary accounting and the comparison that is still missing

All identities (1)--(6) use the original ambient space and hold at
v=0, ell=0 and every zero matrix energy. Formulas (7)--(22) require
ell>0 only where they explicitly divide by ell. For ell>0,v=0,
Section3 gives kstar=kold, so the strict positive-gap improvement
disappears without an invalid cancellation of v. Choices k>kstar
still have rho>0 and provide the calibrated inequalities in Section4.

At ell=0, necessarily v=0,bc=1, so z=0, p=Avec,
Rcut=mu*Q and

                         Gap=K*mu*Q=b*beta*Q.                      (25)

This is the correct undivided identity. It gives no extra prefix
conclusion by division by ell. The old general gap identity and
original full matrix remain the applicable tests. No positive uniform
margin is claimed as ell tends to zero, as v tends to zero, or as
Q,delta,Rcut,Tcut or sigma approach their stated zero cases.

The available shared drafts include the complete compact-domain and
clipping recovery554ec17f7f590bf0, all adjacent and lifted triangles,
the C84--C86 curvature elimination19f1d27c0eb8a588, and the value-price
redundancy17746f8e227d065e. These were read as awareness, not assumed
as accepted mathematical premises of this proof. None currently gives
a point certified to survive their entire conjunction at C>=193/25.
The projected core examples in those drafts are explicitly rejected
by other retained witnesses. They cannot certify independence here.

Consequently this round establishes a new exact three-vector remainder,
its sharp relaxation constant and a matched quadratic calibration
inequality as ONE UNVERIFIED JOINT DRAFT. It does not establish that
the strip (13) or (22) contains a point surviving the complete older
system. Calling this a newly resolved region of that full old system,
or calling its relaxation optimizer a high-capacity seed, would go
beyond the proof. No global emptiness or threshold existence follows.

The concrete next comparison is: on the complete older system and the
original row budget (23), determine the sign of

    k*Rcut-Dprefix-Bcal(delta,Q;sigma,xi_cap)

for the SAME h and an admissible k,theta. A negative value gives a
full original-cone exclusion. If it is always nonnegative on that
entire older system, this calibrated family is redundant there.
Neither alternative is proved. If a point survives, it still requires
the two missing quadratic cross entries in (24), all remaining rows,
and all twenty original signs to be matched simultaneously.

Targeted fact_search found no accepted result for this interface.
Matlas returned unrelated noisy-GD line-search SOS statements; web
fallback recalled already audited composition references. No external
theorem is used. No new literature branch was rejected. The two
direct attempts were first an m-only domination of the old gap, then
the stronger retained-three-vector completion (8), which is the final
result here. The first attempt is subsumed by the latter and is not
counted as an independent advance. Covariance sufficiency was screened
against shared111139417631a944; its already recorded warning remains
applicable, and no extra small-step calibration was constructed.

This is the counterexample part of the same joint draft dd4045b39aa0c257, not a separately accepted building block. Define p_i=sum_(t<i)h_t,S=sum h,D=1+2S,T=sum h_i p_i,d_i=sum_(j<i)h_j(p_i-p_j). For u,v>=0 set prefix lambda_ij=h_i h_j(u+v 1_(i>j)), mu_i=h_i[2+u(Sp_i-T)+v d_i]/(2S-p_i), ell_i=mu_i+v h_i(2p_i+h_i-S),mu_n=2-sum_(i<n)mu_i. Set lambda_(i,star)=mu_i,lambda_(n,i)=ell_i,all star outgoing/incoming terminal zero. In this paragraph these are shortened names for the suffix66 symbols. The manuscript gives an explicit strictly positive choice u=1/T, v=min{1/[4S(S-b)],mu_n^0/(2J)}, with b=min h_i, mu_i^0=h_i(1+Sp_i/T)/(2S-p_i),J=sum h_i d_i/(2S-p_i); mu_n^0=2-sum mu_i^0>0 and J>0. Flow cancels every formal value. The full matrix has initial row (q,-mu^T/2).
At the physical root k=2^(m-1)-1 put r=h_k,left mass A,left square sum Q. Each child has the same enclosing m. Then S=2A+r,T=2A^2+2Ar-Q,E=Ar-Q>0,K=3A+2r,d_k=(A^2+Q)/2,T-E=AS. Symbolic induction gives A>=r and r>4 for all m>=4. Nonnegative terminal multiplier ell_0, with b=h_0, gives uT+2Sv(S-b)<=2. Consequently the actual root minimizer multiplier mu=mu_k=r(2-uE+v d_k)/K obeys mu>=rS/[K(A+r)]>0. The FULL gradient diagonal is Ckk=mu+rA[u(2-r)+v]. Since b<=A, v rA/mu<=AK/S^2<3/4, using 3S^2-4AK=4Ar+3r^2>0. Hence Ckk<7mu/4. Also 10S^2-9K(A+r)=(A-r)(13A+8r)>=0, so Dmu>9r/5.
Now choose an arbitrary unit vector e and assign the ORIGINAL formal coordinates g_k=e, all other g_i=0 including g_n, x0=Dmu e/2. Values have already canceled by flow; no values or interpolation feasibility are assumed. This embedding of a two-coordinate space into the full n+2 coordinates gives M_cert=Ckk-Dmu^2/4<mu(35-9r)/20<-mu/20<0. Thus the full original matrix fails PSD despite all signs and Huber-null equations holding for the explicit rule. For a general level q=chi/D, the same assignment with x0=Dmu e/(2chi) excludes chi<=9r/35. Use h=(a,b,c,d) with1<a<b<2,1/a<beta<1,1/beta<c,d<2, and v>0. Take the one static base function F_base(x,y)=x^2/(2b)+beta*y^2/2, initial(u,v), u>=0. The slow mode p=(1,1-a/b,0,0,0) dies at update1. The fast coordinates z_i=product_(j<i)(1-h_j beta) have signs+,-,+,-,+. Write z3=-t,z4=w, with t,w>0, and B=beta(1-beta)v^2/2>0. At nodes3,4 the slow coordinate is zero. In the fast scalar order, no other sample lies strictly between z3 and z4: node1 is the negative extreme, node2 the positive tail extreme, node0=1 the positive global extreme. Any path crosses the gap[tnegative,wpositive], so the squared-gap bound forces cost at least B(t+w)^2; the direct4->3 edge attains it jointly because both slow coordinates vanish. Thus the complete finite shortest distance is D3=B(t+w)^2. All twenty ordered value inequalities delta_i-delta_j<=w_ij have the simultaneous optimum delta_i=-D_i relative to source4, because distance potentials obey every directed inequality. Since the coefficient2d of(f3-f4) in primitive energy is positive, EVERY value minimizer must satisfy delta4-delta3=D3. If the base origin were also an optimum, its two directed base slacks to3,4 are Bt^2 andBw^2. Summing delta4-delta_star<=Bw^2 anddelta_star-delta3<=Bt^2 yields delta4-delta3<=B(t^2+w^2), a strict contradiction by2Btw>0. This survives every common value shift. There is nevertheless an actual attaining smooth convex function: form Conj_i(y)=<g_i,x_i>-f_i+<x_i,y-g_i>+||y-g_i||^2/2, Conj=max_i Conj_i, F=Conj*. The accepted1252b8823bb5a290 construction interpolates all finite jets; Conj is finite1-strongly convex, so its subgradient set at0 is a nonempty finite convex hull of active(x_i-g_i), exactlyargminF. No fixed optimum is required. The whole new joint energy/price proof is included in research/retained_terminal_four_reversal_redundancy.md; this finding is UNVERIFIED and is not a premise imported into that proof.
