<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001239 -->
# Nonlinear failure of the two-copy ruler despite the quadratic tests

Status: source-based candidate, NOT verifier-accepted. No new solver or
fact_submit. The checkpoint application and projection conversion below
are derived afresh; earlier unverified C27 and C29 are not premises.
The external checkpoint construction is the one explicitly permitted in
PROBLEM.md, Section 7, with its source and applicability checked below.

## Statement

Put c=13/5, a=c^(-17), b_j=a c^j, H_0=(),
H_(j+1)=(H_j,b_j,H_j), and

    s_m=sum H_m=a(c^m-2^m)/(c-2),
    G_m=(H_m,H_m), L_m=2(2^m-1), S_m=2s_m.

There is one fixed integer r such that, for every m>=r+4, an actual
finite-dimensional convex differentiable 1-smooth function f with
f(0)=min f=0, started at a unit vector, satisfies on the whole G_m run

    2f(x_(L_m))
      >= (98/81)^(2^(m-r-2)) / [8(1+s_r)].           (1)

In particular the error grows without bound. For the intrinsic energy

    V_h=2sum_(i<n)h_i(f_i-f_n)
        +sum_(i<n)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
        -S(S+1)||g_n||^2,

the same instance has

    V_(G_m)
      <=1-[s_m/(4(1+s_r))](98/81)^(2^(m-r-2))<0.   (2)

Moreover, a negative-energy instance for this identical ordered schedule
can be realized as F_C(x)=(||x||^2-dist(x,C)^2)/2 on a simplex C with
L_m+2 vertices in dimension L_m+1. The sampled gradients are distinct
vertices, and every normal inequality against a different retained vertex,
including zero, is strict. This last conversion retains the sign of V;
it makes NO assertion preserving (1) after normalizing the new initial
distance.

Together with the independently proved, still unverified quadratic/cut
calculations in C29, this supplies an ACTUAL nonlinear rejection of that
test-safe family. The proof of (1)--(2) does not rely on C29. Neither this
example nor C29 decides primitive capacity for arbitrary schedules or
the original unrestricted Zhang--Jiang exact-profile question.

## 1. External construction, constants, and hypotheses

Source: Yuhan Ye and Kaizhao Liu, Silver Rate Is (Almost) Optimal for
Gradient Descent, arXiv:2609.09152v2, Section 3.1, Lemmas 3.1--3.2 and
the pre-weakening final-value calculation proving Lemma 2.2; proofs in
Appendix A.1--A.3. URL: https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09152v2 . The PDF
and extracted text are in downloads/ye-liu-2609.09152.{pdf,txt}. The
arc construction, projection proof, displacement bound, zero regions,
assembly, and terminal calculation were reread for this application.
A fresh Matlas checkpoint-transfer query returned unrelated fixed-step
results, none used. The direct source's identity and version were checked.

The exact input, also given in PROBLEM.md Section 7, is this. Fix positive
epsilon and gamma, and chi=sqrt(1+2gamma+epsilon^2). There are finite
constants A_YL and B_YL, depending only on these fixed parameters, such
that any nonnegative finite schedule with selected checkpoint sizes
z_i>=B_YL admits a finite-dimensional legal function, minimizer zero,
and initial point e_1, with

    2f(x_n)>=1/[2(1+T)]
               product_i [z_i/(chi(A_YL+u_i)+epsilon z_i)]^2. (3)

Here u_i is the sum after the preceding selected checkpoint and before
checkpoint i, and T is the sum after the last selected checkpoint.
The first u_i includes the whole initial ordinary block.

For completeness, the source's local functions are translated/scaled
parameter-one Moreau envelopes of finite support functions. Their
gradients are projections onto finite convex hulls. The backward arc
construction yields the prescribed trajectory for arbitrary intervening
nonnegative steps and a bounded second-coordinate displacement. For a
local checkpoint b>=1+epsilon^(-2), its first coordinate stops at U>=0,
and the function vanishes both when x<=0,y>=0 and when x=U,y>=Y.
Those regions keep every future and past component inactive during the
appropriate part of the concatenated trajectory. The global function is
half the sum of adjacent-coordinate local functions and a terminal
one-sided Huber function. Each coordinate occurs in at most two terms,
so the global smoothness bound is one. Its local steps are h/2, accounting
for B_YL=2(1+epsilon^(-2)). The initial point is e_1 and all activation
offsets are nonnegative, so zero is a common minimizer. There are one
more coordinates than selected checkpoints. If the final transferred
distance is D, the terminal Huber value is D^2/[4(1+T)], yielding (3)
after multiplication by two. No inference from the paper's notation
for its performance measure is used.

Choose epsilon=1/100 and gamma=2099/20000, so chi=11/10 and
B_YL=20002. Choose any permitted fixed finite A_YL. Then choose a
fixed integer r>=18 sufficiently large that

    b_r>=20002,  chi A_YL/b_r<=1/100.

In particular s_r>1. Indeed s_r/b_r>=13/8 for r>=17:
(10/13)^17<1/40 and (c-2)^(-1)=5/3 give this estimate.
All constants are fixed independently of m.

## 2. The one changed ordinary block at the copy boundary

Choose every step of level at least r in EACH copy of H_m. Put M=m-r.
Within each copy, every ordinary block between selected checkpoints is
exactly H_r. The initial and terminal ordinary blocks are also H_r.
At the junction of the two copies, the one intervening ordinary block
is (H_r,H_r), of sum 2s_r. There is exactly one such exceptional block.
The selected level-ell step b_r c^ell occurs 2^(M-ell-1) times in each
copy, for 0<=ell<M. The terminal ordinary sum of the full word is s_r.

For a usual ordinary block,

    chi(A_YL+s_r)/b_r
       <=1/100+(11/10)(5/3)=553/300<37/20.

Hence each transfer factor at level ell is at least

    q_ell=c^ell/[37/20+c^ell/100].

This increases with ell, and direct rational comparisons give

    q_0>=1/2, q_1>=4/3, q_2>=7/2, q_3>=8;
    q_ell>=1 for ell>=4.

When M>=4 the transfer product over one copy, using ordinary block sum
s_r throughout, is therefore at least

    [(1/2)^8(4/3)^4(7/2)^2 8]^(2^(M-4))
       =(98/81)^(2^(M-4)).                         (4)

For the exceptional block, at its actual following checkpoint z, the
ratio of the actual factor to the factor using ordinary sum s_r is

    [chi(A_YL+s_r)+epsilon z]
      /[chi(A_YL+2s_r)+epsilon z]>=1/2.              (5)

All summands are nonnegative, so the denominator is at most twice the
numerator. The full transfer product is thus at least

    (1/2)(98/81)^(2^(M-3)).

Substituting its square and T=s_r in (3) proves (1). All selected
checkpoints exceed B_YL. The special ordinary block is accepted by the
local construction with its actual doubled chronological step list;
no replacement of that list by a single artificial step is made.

## 3. From the objective blowup to a negative primitive energy

For any normalized legal run with minimizer zero and f(0)=0, smooth
convex interpolation (accepted fact 1252b8823bb5a290) gives

    I_i=<g_i,x_i>-f_i-||g_i||^2/2>=0.

Expanding ||x_(i+1)||^2=||x_i-h_i g_i||^2 and summing gives the exact
identity

    V_h=||x_0||^2-||x_n||^2-2sum_(i<n)h_i I_i
                      -2S f_n-S(S+1)||g_n||^2.

For nonnegative steps and S this is at most ||x_0||^2-2S f_n. Using
||x_0||=1, S=2s_m, and (1) proves the first inequality in (2).
Since s_m>=2^M s_r, M>=4, and s_r>1, its subtracted term is greater
than 4s_r/(1+s_r)>2. Thus (2) is strictly negative at every stated
horizon, not just eventually.

## 4. A finite strict simplex witness, with normalization loss visible

Here is the conversion in full, without invoking an unverified reduction.
Write n=L_m; every step of G_m is positive. Let the energy of the actual
nonlinear run above be -v<0. For ell=0,...,n choose
theta_ell=(ell+1)/(2(n+2)), let A_aux=diag(theta_ell), and run
psi(z)=<z,A_aux z>/2 from z_0=(1,...,1) under the same schedule.
The gradients w_i have components theta_ell P_i(theta_ell), where
P_i(t)=product_(j<i)(1-h_j t). Distinct degrees 0,...,n and n+1
distinct theta_ell imply that the w_i are linearly independent. They
also imply that the z_i are pairwise distinct. Since 0<A_aux<I,

    Q_ij^aux=-(1/2)<z_i-z_j,A_aux(I-A_aux)(z_i-z_j)><0, i!=j,

in the convention Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.

Let E_aux be the finite auxiliary energy and put
delta=v/[2(v+|E_aux|)]. The actual product function
f_mix(x,z)=f(x)+delta psi(z/sqrt(delta)), initialized at
(x_0,sqrt(delta)z_0), has jets
(x_i,sqrt(delta)z_i), (g_i,sqrt(delta)w_i), f_i+delta psi_i.
It remains 1-smooth, its gradient vectors G_i are independent, all its
finite Q_ij are strict, and its energy is at most -v/2. Denote its
points and values by Z_i,F_i for the remainder of this section.

Choose f_* = min_i(F_i-||G_i||^2/2)-1. Independence makes the following
linear equations soluble for z_* in the span of the G_i:

    <G_i,z_*>=f_*-F_i+<G_i,Z_i>-||G_i||^2/2.

Set X_i=Z_i-z_*, V_i=F_i-f_*. Then
V_i=<G_i,X_i>-||G_i||^2/2 and

    <X_i-G_i,G_j-G_i>=Q_ji<0,  i!=j,
    <X_i-G_i,-G_i>=-V_i+||G_i||^2/2<=-1.

Consequently G_i is the projection of X_i onto
C=conv{0,G_0,...,G_n}: the displayed inequalities are the normal
condition against EVERY vertex, hence by convex combinations against
every point of C. Orthogonally project the X_i to the gradient span;
the GD equations, inner products, and all normal inequalities are
unchanged. That span has dimension n+1 and the listed n+2 vertices
are affinely independent.

The function F_C(x)=max_(u in C)(<x,u>-||u||^2/2) is differentiable,
convex, and 1-smooth with gradient P_C: the projection variational
inequalities imply firm nonexpansiveness, and comparison of the two
maxima gives its derivative. It has minimum zero at zero and takes
values V_i at X_i. Translation and common value shifts leave the
intrinsic energy unchanged, so the new energy is at most -v/2.

Finally F_C(X_i)=<G_i,X_i>-||G_i||^2/2 makes every I_i zero, and
expansion of the preceding telescope gives

    V_h=||X_0||^2-||X_n+S G_n||^2<0.

The perturbation and z_* shift need not preserve ||x_0||. Thus the
large normalized objective in (1) belongs to the original source
instance; only the negative sign is asserted for the simplex instance.

This is a symbolic hypothesis test within the same C89 round, not a numerical search or an improving seed proposal. Take F(x,y)=x^2/2+y^2/4, with Hessian A=diag(1,1/2), unique minimizer0, and X_0=(1,1), so the TRUE initial-distance square is R^2=2. Use the three physical GD steps h=(10,20/19,1/2). Exact substitution gives g_0=(1,1/2), X_1=(-9,-4), g_1=(-9,-2), X_2=(9/19,-36/19), g_2=(9/19,-18/19), and X_3=(9/38,-27/19). The gradients at queried samples0 and2 are orthogonal: <g_0,g_2>=9/19-9/19=0. However <g_2,g_1>=-45/19<0. The actual values satisfy 2F(X_2)/R^2=729/722 and 2F(X_3)/R^2=1539/2888, both greater than1/4. Accepted0e7e78056dc6258c defines c_0=0,c_1=4,beta_1=4/(1+sqrt(1+2c_1))^2=1/4. Thus discarding the nonnegative-history hypothesis from the C89 selected-query bound is false; even its terminal consequence fails despite the last step1/2<2. Every original ordered interpolation pair is legal because direct expansion for this quadratic gives Q_ij=-<X_i-X_j,A(I-A)(X_i-X_j)>/2<=0, including the optimum and terminal. The function is convex and1-smooth directly from its diagonal Hessian. This does not refute C89's stated law, whose queried correlations are all nonnegative, and it does not supply an arbitrary-h profile construction. target_claim: extend the selected-query bound without nonnegative earlier correlations; status: refuted at draft level, not verifier accepted; impact: retain the chronological sign hypothesis and leave genuinely signed long-range histories open.

# A complete two-parameter five-step family exclusion at capacity99/10

Status: COMPLETE JOINT RESEARCH DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. This is max3's final
already allocated analytical round, runtime round2 after completed40,
completed ceiling42/attempted43. Latest steering6b4bef299b2b6563 and the
updated TASK.md prioritize parameter geometry. The primary result here
is the complete family analysis in Section3, including a uniform negative
primitive-energy margin. Four further fixed-word witnesses developed
before that steering was read are retained as secondary appendices.

No formal submission, mathematical evaluator, solver, machine algebra,
new numerical proposal, installation, consult or native subagent is used.
The C110 ledger remains CLOSED10 cases with every spare credit retired.
The original exact-profile Goal with W=2R remains OPEN.

Accepted mathematical inputs are04219554e41d9750 Section10 (full original
primitive identity) and1252b8823bb5a290 (interpolation orientation).
Accepted4c92d3fc4eb6153e supplies the conditional threshold context only.
Earlier C110 drafts are awareness, not premises. Every new claim needed
here is derived jointly in this document, with all physical queries,
a static smooth function, every ordered and optimum row, unique minimum,
true radius and original full-cone implication retained.

The appendix labels denote the previously registered words
f5b=(31/20,47/20,5/4,12/5,27/20),
f5d=(31/20,5/2,11/10,12/5,27/20),
f6b=(159/100,5/2,13/10,191/100,5/2,121/100),
f6d=(31/20,49/20,5/4,201/100,5/2,5/4).
Their capacities are99/10 for the five-step words and1201/100 for the
six-step words. All assertions here remain research claims pending the
separately selected independent attack and formal gate.

## 1. Original identity and the certificate implication

For a nonnegative word h of length r, put s=sum_(i<r)h_i and A=1+s.
For actual GD X_(i+1)=X_i-h_i g_i, g_i=grad F(X_i), f_i=F(X_i), set

 Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,X_j-X_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2,
 V_h=2sum_(i<r)h_i(f_i-f_r)
       +sum_(i<r)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g_r||^2.

Retain all formal gradient coordinates0,...,r. For every original array
Lambda with zero diagonal and nonnegative off-diagonal entries, define

 e=(1,...,1)^T,
 T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, T_it=0 for t>i,
 L_flow=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda,
 omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_(r-1),2s)^T,
 D_prim=diag(h_0(2-h_0),...,h_(r-1)(2-h_(r-1)),-s(s+2)),
 M_prim=D_prim+(L_flow T+T^T L_flow^T)/2.

Accepted fact04219554e41d9750 Section10 gives, under L_flow^T e=omega,

 V_h+sum_(i!=j)Lambda_ij Q_ij
       =<M_prim,Gram(g_0,...,g_r)>.

Here Gram is the matrix of all pairwise gradient inner products, and the
matrix inner product is trace of the transpose of the first times the
second. Every actual1-smooth convex trajectory has Q_ij<=0, with this
orientation, by fact1252b8823bb5a290. If V_h<0, the displayed identity
contradicts M_prim>=0 on the FULL R^(r+1). Equality of some gradients in
an actual counterexample deletes no formal coordinate or constraint.

## 2. A jointly proved separated-switch construction

For this section let

 h=(a,b,m_1,...,m_t,c,d),  t in{1,2},
 a,d,m_j>=0, b,c>1, r=t+4.

All names with suffix `sep` and xw,yw below are local defined quantities.
Set

 R_sep=sum_(j=1)^t m_j, B_sep=b-1,
 P_sep=a+b, H_sep=a+b+R_sep+c,
 J_sep=b+R_sep+c+d, C_sep=b+R_sep+c-1, E_sep=c+d,
 tau_sep=(c-1)/E_sep.

Choose k>0 and theta>k^2 and define

 Delta_sep=theta-k^2,
 u_sep=(1,0), v_sep=(-k,sqrt(Delta_sep)),
 xw=(C_sep*k-B_sep)/J_sep, yw=-tau_sep*theta,
 w_sep=(xw,(yw+k*xw)/sqrt(Delta_sep)),
 p_sep=w_sep+u_sep+v_sep, z_sep=w_sep+v_sep,
 beta_sep=-(yw+k*xw)/Delta_sep,
 alpha_sep=k*beta_sep-xw.

Thus <w_sep,u_sep>=xw, <w_sep,v_sep>=yw,
||u_sep||^2=1, ||v_sep||^2=theta and <u_sep,v_sep>=-k.
Assume the following explicit conditions:

 (I) 0<alpha_sep<beta_sep<1;
 (N1) J_sep*yw-B_sep*k+C_sep*theta>=0;
 (N2) E_sep*xw-(c-1)*k<=0;
 (Z1) xw-k<=0;                 (Z2) yw+theta>=0;
 (W1) yw<=0;                  (W2) xw+yw<=0;
 (P1) xw+1-k>=0;              (P2) xw+yw+1-2*k+theta>=0.

The definitions imply w_sep=-alpha_sep*u_sep-beta_sep*v_sep.
Consequently

 0=alpha_sep*p_sep+(beta_sep-alpha_sep)*z_sep
       +(1-beta_sep)*w_sep.

The three coefficients are positive and sum to1. The independent vectors
u_sep,v_sep ensure that the three vertices are affinely independent.
Hence0 is in the interior of K_sep=conv{p_sep,z_sep,w_sep}.

Define on R^2 the SINGLE function

 F(X)=max_(g in K_sep){<X,g>-||g||^2/2}.

Completing the square shows its unique maximizing g is the Euclidean
projection onto K_sep. For projections g,g' of X,X', their normal
inequalities, added together, give

 ||g-g'||^2<=<g-g',X-X'>.

Thus this projection map is1-Lipschitz. The maximum formula gives

 0<=F(X+z)-F(X)-<g,z>
    <=<g(X+z)-g(X),z><=||z||^2,

which proves differentiability with gradient equal to the projection.
F is finite and convex as a maximum of affine functions over a compact
set. Moreover F(0)=0. For X!=0, interiority permits epsilon X in K_sep
for a sufficiently small epsilon in(0,1); then
F(X)>=epsilon(1-epsilon/2)||X||^2>0. Its unique minimizer is therefore0.

Start at

 X_0=A*w_sep+P_sep*u_sep+H_sep*v_sep.

Define candidate gradients at EVERY physical query by

 g_0=g_1=p_sep,
 g_i=z_sep for 2<=i<=r-2,
 g_(r-1)=g_r=w_sep.

Define the X_i recursively using the original steps h_i. The total
weighted gradient is

 (a+b)p_sep+(R_sep+c)z_sep+d*w_sep
   =s*w_sep+P_sep*u_sep+H_sep*v_sep,

so X_r=w_sep. Also X_(r-1)=(1+d)w_sep. Put Y_i=X_i-g_i and define

 Y_b=J_sep*w_sep+B_sep*u_sep+C_sep*v_sep,
 Y_c=E_sep*w_sep+(c-1)*v_sep.

Then Y_1=Y_b, Y_0=Y_b+a*p_sep, Y_(r-2)=Y_c,
Y_(r-1)=d*w_sep, and Y_r=0. For each actual middle query2<=i<=r-3,
let R_i be the sum of its as-yet unused middle steps, including h_i.
Direct subtraction of the physical updates gives Y_i=Y_c+R_i*z_sep.
Each R_i is nonnegative; in particular no middle query is omitted.

Here are ALL vertex normal tests. At p_sep they are
<Y,u_sep>>=0 and <Y,u_sep+v_sep>>=0. At z_sep they are
<Y,u_sep><=0 and <Y,v_sep>>=0. At w_sep they are
<Y,v_sep><=0 and <Y,u_sep+v_sep><=0.

At Y_b, its u_sep product is0 by the definition of xw, and its v_sep
product is N1. Adding a*p_sep preserves both p_sep tests by P1,P2.
At Y_c, its v_sep product is0 by the definition of yw, and its u_sep
product is N2. Adding any R_i*z_sep preserves both z_sep tests by
Z1,Z2. At d*w_sep the two w_sep tests are d times W1,W2; at0 both
vanish. Thus every proposed g_i is exactly the projection of X_i and
is the actual gradient of this SAME function along the entire word.

The exact values are f_i=<X_i,g_i>-||g_i||^2/2. Expanding the ORIGINAL
residual for arbitrary i,j yields

 Q_ij=<g_i-g_j,Y_j><=0,

because g_i belongs to K_sep and Y_j is an outward normal there at g_j.
Include X_star=g_star=f_star=0: then Q_(i,star)=0 and
Q_(star,i)=-<g_i,Y_i><=0, since0 belongs to K_sep. This checks every
ordered remote, terminal and optimum row, with no support restriction.

For these values, telescoping
||X_i||^2-||X_(i+1)||^2=2h_i<X_i,g_i>-h_i^2||g_i||^2
in the definition of V_h, and using X_r=g_r=w_sep, gives

 V_h=||X_0||^2-A^2||w_sep||^2
    =P_sep^2-2k*P_sep*H_sep+theta*H_sep^2
       +2A(P_sep*xw+H_sep*yw).                         (E)

The true squared initial distance is exactly ||X_0||^2 because the
unique minimizer is0. More explicitly, its exact value is

 (A*xw+P_sep-H_sep*k)^2
 +[A*(yw+k*xw)/sqrt(Delta_sep)+H_sep*sqrt(Delta_sep)]^2.

Every assertion in this construction was derived in this joint argument;
none is an imported unverified lemma.

## 3. Complete two-parameter family exclusion at the verified threshold

Consider the SINGLE physical five-step family

 h(a,d)=(a,12/5,41/10-a-d,12/5,d),
 a>=0, d>=0, a+d<=41/10.                               (F)

Every word has capacity A=99/10. We now give a joint exact proof that
NO word in this family admits the full original30-multiplier/R6 PSD
primitive certificate. This is the principal parameter-level result of
this round; it includes the original screen-passing f5a word.

### 3.1 Original scalar cuts reduce the entire family

For any selected physical step q_cut>1, define C_cut=1+sum of the
preceding steps and D_cut=1+sum of the following steps. Let
eta_cut=(q_cut-1)/D_cut and define

 F_cut(x)=max_(g in[-eta_cut,1]){xg-g^2/2}.

Its gradient is clip(x,[-eta_cut,1]), so it is convex differentiable
1-smooth with unique minimizer0. Start at X_0=C_cut. Every query before
and at q_cut is at least1, with gradient1, and the query at q_cut is1.
The step q_cut lands at -(q_cut-1). Every subsequent query is
-eta_cut times(1+the remaining suffix sum), with gradient -eta_cut,
and the terminal point is -eta_cut. Thus all original physical updates
hold. The projection normal formula gives every ordered residual
Q_ij=(g_i-g_j)(X_j-g_j)<=0, and both optimum directions are also
nonpositive. The exact true squared radius is C_cut^2. Substitution
or the norm telescoping in Section2 gives

 V_h=C_cut^2-A^2*eta_cut^2.

A full original certificate must therefore satisfy

 A(q_cut-1)<=C_cut*D_cut.                              (C)

For the first12/5 step in(F), inequality(C) reads

 (1+a)(15/2-a)>=693/50,
 equivalently (a-6/5)(a-53/10)<=0.

Since0<=a<=41/10<53/10, this forces a>=6/5. The second12/5 step
gives the same inequality with d in place of a, forcing d>=6/5.
We may then apply(C) to the first and last steps, which exceed1.
They give (A+1)a<=2A and (A+1)d<=2A. Every possible full certificate
in(F) must consequently lie in

 6/5<=a,d<=198/109<11/6.                              (R)

Outside(R) at least one actual scalar witness just constructed has
negative primitive energy. Inside the larger closed rectangle

 6/5<=a,d<=11/6,                                     (Rplus)

the middle step is at least13/30>0, so all five steps are valid.

### 3.2 One rational triangle works over the whole rectangle

In the construction of Section2 use t=1, b=c=12/5,
m_1=41/10-a-d, and FIX the geometry parameters

 k=3/10, theta=1/4, Delta_sep=4/25,
 u_sep=(1,0), v_sep=(-3/10,2/5).

All vertices and iterates are thus rational functions of a,d. Introduce
S_sep=A+B_sep=113/10, with B_sep=7/5. The earlier parameters become

 P_sep=a+12/5, E_sep=d+12/5,
 J_sep=S_sep-P_sep, H_sep=S_sep-E_sep,
 C_sep=J_sep-E_sep+B_sep,
 D_sep(E_sep)=k*E_sep+B_sep*(1-k),
 xw=k-D_sep(E_sep)/J_sep, yw=-B_sep*theta/E_sep.

On(Rplus) their exact ranges are

 18/5<=P_sep,E_sep<=127/30,
 106/15<=J_sep,H_sep<=77/10,
 103/50<=D_sep(E_sep)<=9/4,
 -39/2120<=xw<=5/154,
 -7/72<=yw<=-21/254.

In particular

 -1/50<xw<1/30,  -1/10<yw<-2/25.                     (B)

Here are complete uniform checks of all eight normal conditions.
For Z1,Z2,W1,W2,P1,P2 respectively, bounds(B) give

 xw-k<-4/15,
 yw+theta>3/20,
 yw<-2/25,
 xw+yw<-7/150,
 xw+1-k>17/25,
 xw+yw+1-2*k+theta>53/100.

For N2, E_sep>0, E_sep<=127/30 and xw<1/30 give

 E_sep*xw-(c-1)k<127/900-21/50=-251/900.

For N1, substitution yields

 J_sep*yw-B_sep*k+C_sep*theta
  =[(E_sep-B_sep)(J_sep-E_sep)/E_sep]/4-21/50.

The bracket is increasing in J_sep. At J_sep=106/15 its derivative
with respect to E_sep is -1+(106/15)(7/5)/E_sep^2<0, since
(106/15)(7/5)=742/75<(18/5)^2. Its minimum on the rectangle is
therefore at J_sep=106/15,E_sep=127/30, where it is1445/762.
It follows that N1>=1445/3048-21/50=4121/76200>0.

For interiority, bounds(B) imply

 7/16<beta_sep=-(yw+(3/10)xw)/(4/25)<53/80<1.

Then

 alpha_sep=(3/10)beta_sep-xw>47/480>0,
 beta_sep-alpha_sep=(7/10)beta_sep+xw>229/800>0.

Thus all three barycentric coefficients are positive throughout(Rplus).
The static function, every physical iterate, all remote and optimum
rows, and the unique minimizer are exactly those proved in Section2.
Also

 <X_0,u_sep>=A*xw+P_sep-k*H_sep
   >-99/500+18/5-(3/10)(77/10)=273/250>0.

Hence the true initial distance is nonzero everywhere, and its precise
value is the squared-coordinate formula in Section2. No gradient
coordinate or original certificate constraint is removed.

### 3.3 Exact uniform primitive-energy sign

Let V_family(P_sep,E_sep) be the value of(E) for the parameters above:

 V_family=P_sep^2-2k*P_sep*H_sep+theta*H_sep^2
      +2A[P_sep*(k-D_sep(E_sep)/J_sep)
               -B_sep*theta*H_sep/E_sep],
 J_sep=S_sep-P_sep, H_sep=S_sep-E_sep.                 (V)

We bound(V) over the ENTIRE rectangle, without a numerical evaluation.
All derivatives here are ordinary derivatives of the displayed rational
function on positive J_sep,E_sep. Differentiating gives

 dV_family/dP_sep
   =2[P_sep+k(E_sep-B_sep)]
          -2A*S_sep*D_sep(E_sep)/J_sep^2,
 d^2V_family/dP_sep^2
   =2-4A*S_sep*D_sep(E_sep)/J_sep^3,
 d^2V_family/(dP_sep dE_sep)
   =2k[1-A*S_sep/J_sep^2].

The second derivative in P_sep is negative: its required inequality
4A*S_sep*D_sep>2J_sep^3 follows throughout the rectangle from

 4A*S_sep*(103/50)-2(77/10)^3=43714/5000>0.

The mixed derivative is negative because
A*S_sep=11187/100>(77/10)^2=5929/100. Consequently dV_family/dP_sep
is minimized at P_sep=E_sep=127/30. At that corner D_sep=9/4,
J_sep=106/15, and its exact value is

 61/6-22653675/2247200=23143/269664>0.

Thus V_family is increasing in P_sep on the rectangle, so its maximum
is bounded by the one-variable curve with P_sep=127/30.

For that curve, direct differentiation gives

 d^2V_family/dE_sep^2
    =1/2-A*B_sep*S_sep/E_sep^3<0.

Indeed E_sep<5, while A*B_sep*S_sep=78309/500>125. Hence its graph
lies below its tangent at E_sep=4, an interior point of the interval.
The tangent data, obtained by substituting in(V) and its derivative, are

 V_family(127/30,4)=-1254163/1908000,
 (dV_family/dE_sep)(127/30,4)=191577/848000>0.

For additional arithmetic transparency, at this point
xw=-9/1060, yw=-7/80, H_sep=73/10;
the first three terms of(V) are228629/18000, and
P_sep*xw+H_sep*yw=-28607/42400. These give the displayed value.
The derivative is -2159/2120-73/20+78309/16000, giving the displayed
positive slope.

Since E_sep<=127/30=4+7/30, the tangent inequality yields the uniform
strict bound

 V_h=V_family(P_sep,E_sep)
   <=-1254163/1908000+(191577/848000)(7/30)
    =-46143403/76320000
    =-3/5-351403/76320000<-3/5.                        (NEG)

Thus EVERY word in(Rplus), and therefore every possible scalar-cut
survivor(R), has an actual global smooth-convex trajectory with strictly
negative original primitive energy. Combined with the scalar witnesses
outside(R), this proves the asserted exclusion of the ENTIRE family(F)
in the full original cone, as a joint research draft.

### 3.4 Strength, survivor direction and scope

This is stronger than just the scalar global cuts. At the family member
a=3/2,d=7/5 (the registered f5a word), the five original scalar-cut
slacks C_cut*D_cut-A(h_i-1) are, in physical order,

 69/20, 57/50, 1077/50, 39/50, 227/50,

all positive. Its unit-curvature scalar-quadratic energy also passes:
product_i(1-h_i)=-49/625, so
1-A^2 product_i(1-h_i)^2=15530299/39062500>0.
Yet(NEG) excludes its full original primitive certificate. No claim is
made that passing these screens suffices elsewhere.

There is NO survivor within the fixed family(F) at capacity99/10,
subject to verification of this joint proof. Retuning either endpoint
or the nonnegative middle step while retaining both12/5 peaks and the
same total cannot supply the missing seed. A future seed at this exact
capacity must change this physical pattern; the argument makes no
claim about independently varied peak heights, different ordering,
other capacities, or six-step families. No actual seed has been found.
In particular this is not an all-length-five no-seed theorem, and it
is not the unrestricted original minimax lower bound.

## 5. Appendix: exact energies for the four words

Formula(E) has the following components and exact values:

| 2A | 99/5 | 99/5 | 1201/50 | 1201/50 |

=97207835830-98314677881=-1106842051.
All denominators are positive and all numerators negative. Section1
therefore excludes every original full-PSD primitive certificate for
each displayed word. There are30 free off-diagonal multipliers and6
formal gradients for each five-step word, and42 multipliers and7
formal gradients for each six-step word. No choice of their support,
symmetry, rank or nonzero PSD remainder repairs these fixed words.

## 6. Appendix: failed geometry choices and their repairs

The former common choice k=2/5,theta=1/4 is NOT legal for f5d:
its first-switch product is -9/250<0. The revised k=3/8,theta=1/4

For f6d, k=2/5,theta=1/4 would keep xw=717/4730 but set yw=-1/10.

These are specific failed geometries, not exclusions of the full
construction family. No numerical search was used to select them.

## 7. General interface and final scope

Eliminating xw,yw in(E) gives the affine expression

 V_h=P_sep^2-2A*P_sep*B_sep/J_sep
       +2P_sep*(A*C_sep/J_sep-H_sep)*k
       +H_sep*(H_sep-2A*tau_sep)*theta.                 (A)

Thus, for any pair(k,theta) satisfying(I) and the eight sign tests,
nonnegativity of(A) is a NECESSARY condition for a full original
primitive certificate. This conditional analytical test is a consequence
of the same joint construction, not a new standalone accepted fact.
It need not depend on the distribution of the nonnegative middle steps:
their sum determines all parameters, and their partial sums only enter
as the nonnegative coefficients R_i. No converse or completeness of
this test is claimed, and failure of one parameter pair proves nothing.

The earlier final C110 joint draft gave exact research counterexamples
for f5a,f5c,f6a,f6c. This draft separately handles its four remaining
words f5b,f5d,f6b,f6d. Therefore all eight registered proposals now have
explicit exact obstruction DRAFTS in the shared evidence. Neither this
statement nor the earlier four proofs has passed a new formal gate.
Do not cite the eight-word conclusion as a fact or infer an entire-family
or unrestricted seed-impossibility theorem.

There is still no actual full primitive seed at the accepted capacities,
and the original exact-profile Goal with W=2R remains OPEN. The numerical
budget is unchanged and CLOSED at10 cases; the unused2 slots and seconds
stay retired. No formal verifier allocation has been granted. This
finishes the already allocated final analytical round and does not renew

# All finite clipping ratios: complete-child capture after every return

**ONE JOINT UNVERIFIED ANALYTICAL REPORT.** Project
`gd-exact-profile-20260926`, worker max3, invocation77 after baseline76,
completed ceiling77 / attempted ceiling78, controller cap1. This is a
single proof candidate, not a verified fact. No fact_submit or other
formal call was authorized or made. No mathematical program, numerical
evaluation, trajectory replay, optimizer, recurrence evaluation, consult,
installation or subagent was used.

The candidate closes the previously missing intermediate ratios for the
specified unit-core scalar clipping family. It does not upper-bound the
original full function-class supremum W and does not resolve the original
minimax target. All new assertions, including supporting assertions,
belong to this one joint unverified derivation.

## 1. Exact candidate and scope

Fix any integer m>=1536, once and for the entire word. Define

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon,
    c=lambda*(1+epsilon), a=beta/c, d_j=a*c^j,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+d_j,H_j), 0<=j<m,
    S_j=sum H_j, A_j=1+S_j, D_j=1+2*S_j,
    n_m=2^m-1, ell=1+a, b=a*beta.

Hierarchy a,c,H_j and the map notation below are LOCAL notation. In
particular H_j here is the physical child word, not the conjugate jet
quadratic named H_j in accepted1252. There is no child parameter reset.

For any fixed u,v>0, put

    g(x)=clip(x,[-v,u]),
    f(x)=x*g(x)-g(x)^2/2.

Let Psi_j be the actual chronological GD map of the entire H_j on this
ONE static function. The candidate is

    for EVERY u,v>0 and EVERY s!=0,
        2*f(Psi_m(s)) <= s^2/D_m;                    (1.1)
    sup_(s!=0) 2*f(Psi_m(s))/s^2 = 1/D_m.            (1.2)

Both s=D_m*u and s=-D_m*v attain equality. The statement includes
arbitrary dependence of u/v on m, both initial signs, every initial
amplitude and the literal final physical leaf.

Writing the exact interval image as

    Psi_m([-R,R])=[-p_m(R,R),q_m(R,R)],

the two endpoint conclusions, for EVERY R>0, are

    2*f(-p_m(R,R)) <= R^2/D_m,
    2*f( q_m(R,R)) <= R^2/D_m.                       (1.3)

The original quantity remains

    W_n(h)=sup 2*(F(x_n)-F(x_star))/(L*||x_0-x_star||^2),
    x_(i+1)=x_i-(h_i/L)*grad F(x_i), W_n(h)=2*R_n(h),

over every permitted finite dimension and smooth convex function with
an attained minimum, with every original n and nonnegative h retained.
The target W_n^star/U_n^ZJ ->1, or a rigorous negative resolution of it,
is unchanged. PROBLEM.md is unchanged. A restricted-family equality
such as(1.2) is not an upper bound on W_(n_m)(H_m).

Accepted fact_id `1252b8823bb5a290` supplies the original normalization,
the ordered residual convention and the distinction between actual
scalar rows and full formal-space certificates. No full certificate is
claimed here. Accepted fact_id `b98bb807d514e80d` is used only for the
original benchmark terminology and single-cut context; its LOWER bound
is never reversed. The all-position estimate needed here is proved in
Section8. Accepted fact_id `931b9653ce0f7201` supplies the unconditional
UPPER comparison B_(n_m)/D_m<47/50, used only at the end. Its full-class
hypothesis17/(16D_m) remains unproved.

TASK.md and master_guidance `9f1dcb56a7b38fea` govern this allocation.
Plan `40cf581f6e285416` records the new mechanism. Prior reports
`05423f48b823e3bf` and `72141426b777f11c` were read as awareness,
not accepted premises. Every prior-draft premise used below is derived
again inside this joint candidate.

## 2. Recovered domain and the new mechanism

The recovered prior state was: the uniform band

    max(u,v)/min(u,v) <= 1/(c-2),

the entire tail

    max(u,v)/min(u,v) >= Gamma_m,
    Gamma_m=sqrt(2*a*d_(m-1)*D_m),

all zero/single-sign-crossing histories, and the finite-clipping transfer
for u>=d_(m-1)*v with s<=u, had joint unverified derivations. The missing
domain lay between the band and Gamma_m; below d_(m-1) the upper clip
can reactivate repeatedly. None of those prior claims was an accepted
fact. The old one-sided tail proof is not repeated as a new result.

The NEW mechanism proves for u>=2v, simultaneously for every complete
child H_j, a weak global radial bound and a sharp two-endpoint plateau:

    |Psi_j(s)| <= max(v, |s|-S_j*v/3),               (2.1)
    Phi_j(d_j*v*b^t,d_j*v*b^t)
        <= (v*b^(t+1),v*b^(t+1)), t=0,1,2,... .     (2.2)

Phi_j is the exact signed interval map; inequalities are coordinatewise.
The radial bound(2.1) is deliberately much weaker than the false
primitive-energy inequality. Its role is to prove(2.2), not to certify
the terminal objective by itself.

A negative-to-positive return at a level-j bridge lands at most d_j*v.
The following full H_j therefore captures it by(2.2). The actual suffix
then consists of increasing ancestor levels, each followed by its own
complete right child. Repeated reactivation inside any such child is
already included in the joint induction. This gives a closed terminal
bound, not merely a recursive selector. The previously established small
band, jointly rederived in Section9, overlaps u/v>=2 and completes all
ratios. This is one substantive mechanism; no second search law was
activated.

## 3. Parameter inequalities and literal word structure

Here epsilon<=1/6144<1/100, beta>99/100. Since sqrt(2)<29/20,

    2<c<(49/20)*(101/100)<5/2,
    1/3<99/250<a<1/lambda<1/2,
    0<b<1/2, c*b=beta^2<1.                         (3.1)

Let eta=a/(c-2). From lambda*(lambda-2)=1 and c>lambda we get
c*(c-2)>1 and 0<eta<1. Summing the physical concatenation gives

    A_(j+1)=2*A_j+d_j,
    A_j=(1-eta)*2^j+eta*c^j,
    d_j/A_j<=c-2<1/2,
    A_j<c^j for j>=1.                              (3.2)

The closed formula has initial value1, and its update follows from
2*eta+a=c*eta. In particular S_j=A_j-1, A_j>=A_1=2+a>2 for j>=1,
and

    d_j<S_j for every j>=1.                         (3.3)

Indeed d_j<A_j/2<A_j-1 because A_j>2. The d_j increase strictly;
d_0=a<1, d_1=beta<1, and d_j>1 for j>=2 because d_2=beta*c>1.

For the small-band proof we also need, for 2<=j<=m,

    d_j>A_(j-1),
    (c-1)*d_(j-1)<A_(j-1).                          (3.4)

For the first inequality at j=2, expansion gives

    c*(d_2-A_1)=2*epsilon-lambda^2*epsilon^2*(1+epsilon)>0,

since lambda^2<6 and 6*epsilon*(1+epsilon)<2. If d_j>A_(j-1),
then, using A_j=2*A_(j-1)+d_j/c,

    d_(j+1)-A_j=(c-1/c)*d_j-2*A_(j-1)>0,

as c-1/c>lambda-1/lambda=2. The second inequality in(3.4) follows
from (c-1)*(d_(j-1)/A_(j-1)) <= (c-1)*(c-2)<3/4.

At the final level,

    d_m^2>D_m.                                      (3.5)

Indeed d_m^2>c^(2m)/9 and D_m<2*c^m, while c^m>2^m>=32>18 for
m>=5. This covers all stated m. d_m is only an auxiliary ruler; the
largest physical bridge is d_(m-1).

H_j has2^j-1 actual steps, by induction on its defining concatenation.
Each occurrence of a level-k bridge inside H_j has an actual H_k
immediately before and immediately after it. Thus its physical prefix
and suffix masses inside H_j are each at least S_k. All steps exceed1.
The first and final steps of every nonempty H_j are the leaf ell=1+a.

A further exact word property is needed. After a chosen level-k bridge,
first consume its following H_k. This completes its canonical H_(k+1)
block. If any word remains, ascend past every ancestor for which this
was a right child. The next step is the bridge of the first ancestor
for which the completed block lies on the left. Its level is larger
than k, and its full right child follows it. Repeating gives the suffix

    H_k, (1+d_(l_1)),H_(l_1), ...,
         (1+d_(l_r)),H_(l_r),                       (3.6)

where k<l_1<...<l_r<=m-1. The list may be empty. This is a
decomposition of the literal binary concatenation tree, not a deletion
or permutation of steps. At k=0 the first H_0 is empty. If the chosen
step is the last leaf, there is no subsequent step.
